```
│ 4. Context Eng. │  │ 3. Tool Use Layer│  │ 8. Hooks System│
│                 │  │                  │  │  (生命周期钩子) │
└────────┬────────┘  └────────┬─────────┘  └────────────────┘
         │                    │
         ▼                    ▼
┌─────────────────┐  ┌──────────────────┐
│ 5. Memory System│  │ 9. Permission    │ ◄── 叶节点 (仅依赖 L9 基础设施)
│                 │  │    Gateway       │
└────────┬────────┘  └──────────────────┘
         │                    ▲
         └────────────────────┤
                              │ (Permission 被以下模块依赖)
         ┌────────────────────┼────────────────────┐
         │                    │                    │
         ▼                    ▼                    ▼
┌─────────────────┐  ┌──────────────────┐  ┌────────────────┐
│ 2. Skill System │  │ 6. Plugin System │  │ 7. MCP Integr. │
│ (依赖 Tool+Perm)│  │ (依赖 Tool+Perm) │  │ (依赖 Tool+Perm)│
└────────┬────────┘  └────────┬─────────┘  └────────┬───────┘
         │                    │                     │
         └────────────────────┼─────────────────────┘
                              │
                              ▼  (Skill/Plugin/MCP 被以下模块依赖)
                   ┌──────────────────────┐
                   │ 10. Subagent Manager │ ── - -► IAgentLoop (接口反转)
                   │    (派生子 Agent)     │
                   └──────────┬───────────┘
                              │
                              ▼
                   ┌──────────────────────┐
                   │11. Multi-agent       │
                   │    Coordinator       │
                   └──────────────────────┘
```

**关键依赖路径** (单向，无环):
1. `Agent Loop (1) ──► Tool Use Layer (3) ──► Permission Gateway (9)` — Loop 执行工具前经权限网关
2. `Agent Loop (1) ──► Context Engine (4) ──► Memory System (5)` — Loop 组装上下文时读取记忆
3. `Subagent Manager (10) ──► Skill (2) / Plugin (6) / MCP (7) ──► Tool (3) ──► Permission (9)` — 子 Agent 复用能力扩展
4. `Multi-agent Coordinator (11) ──► Subagent Manager (10)` — 多智能体协调器调度子 Agent

#### 8.2.1 循环依赖打破策略 (v4.2 新增, S5)

**问题**: `Agent Loop (1)` 调用 `Subagent Manager (10)` 派生子 Agent；而 `Subagent Manager (10)` 派生子 Agent 时需要创建子循环 (sub-loop)，又需要调用 `Agent Loop (1)`。这形成 **Loop ↔ Subagent 循环依赖**。

**打破策略 — 依赖倒置 (Dependency Inversion)**:

引入 `IAgentLoop` 接口 (定义在 `@openwork/api` 契约层)，`Subagent Manager` 依赖该**接口**而非 `Agent Loop` 模块的具体实现。`Agent Loop Engine` 实现 `IAgentLoop` 接口，在运行时由 BackendService 注入到 `SubagentManager`。

```typescript
// packages/api/src/agent-loop.ts (契约层，无实现)
export interface IAgentLoop {
  /** 派生子循环 (Subagent 调用此方法，不依赖 Loop Engine 具体实现) */
  spawnSubLoop(input: TaskInput, opts: SubLoopOptions): AsyncIterable<AgentEvent>;
  /** 暂停子循环 */
  pauseSubLoop(loopId: string): Promise<Checkpoint>;
  /** 从 Checkpoint 恢复子循环 */
  resumeSubLoop(loopId: string, cp: Checkpoint): AsyncIterable<AgentEvent>;
}

// packages/core/src/agent/loop/loop-engine.ts (实现层)
export class LoopEngine implements IAgentLoop {
  spawnSubLoop(input: TaskInput, opts: SubLoopOptions): AsyncIterable<AgentEvent> {
    // 创建子循环，复用 LoopEngine 内部能力
  }
}

// packages/core/src/agent/subagent/manager.ts (依赖接口，非具体实现)
export class SubagentManager {
  constructor(private loopFactory: () => IAgentLoop) {}
  // SubagentManager 通过 IAgentLoop 接口派生子循环，不直接 import LoopEngine
}
```

**依赖方向** (打破循环后):
- `SubagentManager (10) ──► IAgentLoop (接口, @openwork/api)` — 接口依赖 (虚线)
- `LoopEngine (1) implements IAgentLoop` — 实现关系
- `BackendService (L5)` 负责在启动时将 `LoopEngine` 实例注入 `SubagentManager` (依赖注入)

这样 `SubagentManager` 不再直接依赖 `LoopEngine` 模块，循环依赖被打破，编译期与运行期均为单向依赖 DAG (有向无环图)。

> **注**: `Multi-agent Coordinator (11)` 同样通过 `IAgentLoop` 接口调度多 Agent，不直接依赖 `LoopEngine`。`Hooks System (8)` 通过 Hook 点被 `LoopEngine` 调用 (反向控制)，不形成循环。

---

### 8.3 模块 1: Agent Loop Engine

#### 8.3.1 模块定位与职责

Loop Engine 是 Agent 的「心跳」。它定义 Agent 如何从感知到行动、何时停止、如何反思、如何把大任务分解为可执行的子循环。

**核心职责**:
- 执行 PAOR (Perceive-Act-Observe-Reflect) 主循环
- 调度 5 个子循环 (Planning / Execution / Verification / Reflection / Recovery)
- 中断与恢复 (Checkpoint 序列化)
- HITL (Human-In-The-Loop) 干预点管理
- 最大迭代与成本上限守护

#### 8.3.2 核心抽象/接口

```typescript
// src/agent/loop/loop-engine.ts
export interface LoopEngine {
  /** 启动 Agent 循环 */
  run(input: TaskInput): AsyncIterable<AgentEvent>;

  /** 暂停 (返回 Checkpoint) */
  pause(): Promise<Checkpoint>;

  /** 从 Checkpoint 恢复 */
  resume(cp: Checkpoint): AsyncIterable<AgentEvent>;

  /** 单步执行 (调试) */
  step(): Promise<StepResult>;

  /** 获取当前状态 */
  inspect(): AgentState;

  /** 订阅事件流 */
  subscribe(): AsyncIterable<AgentEvent>;

  /** 中断当前循环 */
  interrupt(reason: InterruptReason): Promise<void>;
}

export interface SubLoopScheduler {
  shouldTrigger(loopType: SubLoopType, ctx: LoopContext): boolean;
  trigger(loopType: SubLoopType, ctx: LoopContext): Promise<SubLoopResult>;
  onComplete(result: SubLoopResult): LoopAction;
}

export type SubLoopType =
  | 'planning'
  | 'execution'
  | 'verification'
  | 'reflection'
  | 'recovery';

export interface Checkpoint {
  id: string;
  sessionId: string;
  step: number;
  phase: LoopPhase;
  messageHistory: Message[];
  workingMemory: Map<string, unknown>;
  taskPlan?: TaskPlan;
  tokenUsage: TokenUsage;
  costUsd: number;
  createdAt: number;
}

export type LoopPhase = 'perceive' | 'act' | 'observe' | 'reflect';
export type LoopStatus = 'idle' | 'running' | 'paused' | 'completed' | 'error';

export interface AgentState {
  sessionId: string;
  loopStatus: LoopStatus;
  currentStep: number;
  currentPhase: LoopPhase;
  tokensUsed: TokenUsage;
  costUsd: number;
  subAgentsActive: number;
  pendingToolCalls: ToolCallId[];
}
```

#### 8.3.3 PAOR 主循环实现

```typescript
// src/agent/loop/paor-loop.ts
export class PaorLoop implements LoopEngine {
  constructor(
    private prompt: PromptEngine,
    private context: ContextEngine,
    private harness: Harness,
    private model: ModelProvider,
    private hooks: HooksRuntime,
    private subAgents: SubAgentManager,
    private db: Database,
    private config: LoopConfig,
  ) {}

  async *run(input: TaskInput): AsyncIterable<AgentEvent> {
    let step = 0;
    let plan: TaskPlan | undefined;

    while (step < this.config.maxIterations) {
      step++;
      yield { type: 'step:start', step, phase: 'perceive' };

      // === P: Perceive ===
      const ctx = await this.context.collect(input.sources);
      const compressed = await this.context.compress(
        input.history,
        this.config.budget,
      );
      const prompt = await this.prompt.assemble({ input, ctx: compressed });
      await this.hooks.execute('pre_model_call', { prompt, step });

      // 子循环: Planning (复杂任务触发)
      if (this.shouldPlan(input, step) && !plan) {
        plan = await this.runSubLoop('planning', { input, ctx });
        yield { type: 'plan:created', plan };
        if (this.config.requireUserApprovePlan) {
          const approved = await this.requestUserApproval(plan);
          if (!approved) return;
        }
      }

      // === A: Act ===
      yield { type: 'step:act', step };
      const stream = this.model.chat(prompt);
      const result = await this.consumeStream(stream, step);
      await this.hooks.execute('post_model_call', { result, step });
      yield { type: 'message:assistant', content: result.content };

      if (result.toolCalls.length === 0) {
        // 无工具调用 → 任务可能完成
        if (await this.isTaskComplete(result)) {
          yield { type: 'task:complete', step };
          break;
        }
        continue;
      }

      // === O: Observe ===
      for (const call of result.toolCalls) {
        const hookResult = await this.hooks.execute('pre_act', { call });
        if (hookResult.action === 'block') {
          yield { type: 'tool:blocked', call, reason: hookResult.reason };
          continue;
        }
        const toolResult = await this.harness.dispatchTool(call);
        await this.hooks.execute('on_tool_result', { call, result: toolResult });
        yield { type: 'tool:result', call, result: toolResult };
      }

      // === R: Reflect (每 N 轮) ===
      if (step % this.config.reflectionInterval === 0) {
        const reflection = await this.runSubLoop('reflection', { step });
        yield { type: 'reflection', reflection };

        // 子循环: Verification (代码改动后)
        if (this.hasCodeChanges(result.toolCalls)) {
          const verify = await this.runSubLoop('verification', { step });
          if (!verify.passed) {
            await this.runSubLoop('recovery', { failure: verify });
          }
        }
      }

      // 保存 Checkpoint
      await this.saveCheckpoint(step, input);
      yield { type: 'step:end', step };
    }
  }

  private async runSubLoop(type: SubLoopType, ctx: LoopContext): Promise<SubLoopResult> {
    // 子循环通过专门的 Prompt 模板执行
    const skillPrompt = this.config.subLoopTemplates[type];
    const result = await this.model.chatSync(skillPrompt.render(ctx));
    return this.parseSubLoopResult(type, result);
  }
}
```

#### 8.3.4 子循环触发规则

| 子循环 | 触发条件 | 行为 |
|--------|----------|------|
| **Planning** | 预计步数 > 3 / 复杂任务 (多文件/多步骤) | 先产出 Plan (TodoList + 依赖 DAG)，再执行 |
| **Execution** | Plan 中的子任务 | 串行/并行执行单个子任务 |
| **Verification** | 代码改动后 / 阶段完成 | 跑测试 / 类型检查 / Lint / 自查 |
| **Reflection** | 每 N 轮 (默认 5) 或阶段完成 | 自评，必要时重规划 |
| **Recovery** | 工具失败 / 测试失败 | 分析错误，尝试修复，最多 K 次 (默认 3) |

#### 8.3.5 中断与恢复

```typescript
export interface CheckpointManager {
  save(sessionId: string, state: AgentState): Promise<Checkpoint>;
  load(checkpointId: string): Promise<Checkpoint>;
  listLatest(sessionId: string, n: number): Promise<Checkpoint[]>;
  prune(sessionId: string, keepLast: number): Promise<void>;
}

export interface HitlMediator {
  requestApproval(action: HitlAction): Promise<ApprovalResult>;
  registerBreakpoint(bp: Breakpoint): void;
  removeBreakpoint(id: string): void;
  hitBreakpoint(step: number, phase: LoopPhase): Promise<BreakpointAction>;
}

export type HitlAction = {
  type: 'tool_call' | 'plan_approval' | 'destructive_op';
  toolId?: string;
  args?: unknown;
  description: string;
  riskLevel: RiskLevel;
};

export type Breakpoint = {
  id: string;
  step?: number;           // 指定步数
  toolId?: string;         // 指定工具调用前
  condition?: string;      // 条件表达式
  action: 'pause' | 'ask';
};
```

#### 8.3.6 关键设计决策

| ID | 决策 | 理由 |
|----|------|------|
| D6.1.1 | 采用 PAOR 而非纯 ReAct | 显式 Reflect 阶段提升复杂任务成功率 |
| D6.1.2 | 子循环可配置开关 | 不同任务最优策略不同，避免一刀切 |
| D6.1.3 | Loop 状态可序列化为 Checkpoint | 长任务不丢失，支持时间旅行回放 |
| D6.1.4 | Doom Loop 检测: 连续 3 次相同工具调用触发警告 | 防止死循环烧钱 (参考 OpenCode) |
| D6.1.5 | Checkpoint 频率可配 (每步/每 N 步/按需) | 平衡持久化开销与数据丢失风险 |

#### 8.3.7 配置项

```yaml
loop:
  max_iterations: 50
  max_iterations_per_subtask: 15
  reflection_interval: 5
  doom_loop_threshold: 3       # 连续相同调用次数告警
  planning:
    auto_plan_threshold: 3
    require_user_approve_plan: false
  verification:
    auto_run_tests: true
    auto_lint: true
    auto_typecheck: true
  recovery:
    max_retries: 3
    on_persistent_failure: "ask"  # ask | abort | escalate
  hitl:
    mode: "key_nodes"           # every_step | key_nodes | trust
    confirm_on: [delete, run_command, force_push, irreversible]
  checkpoint:
    enabled: true
    interval: "every_step"      # every_step | every_n | on_demand
    keep_last: 20
  trace:
    enabled: true
    capture_observations: true
```

---

### 8.4 模块 2: Skill System

#### 8.4.1 模块定位与职责

Skill 是「可复用的 Prompt + 工具白名单 + 工作流 + 可选脚本」封装。与「插件」区别: Skill 主要是 prompt/workflow 层扩展，不引入新代码运行时。

**核心职责**:
- 内置 Skills 库 (TDD / commit / PR / code-review / debug / refactor / scaffold / docs 等)
- 用户自定义 Skills (YAML)
- 意图匹配与自动调用
- 工具白名单隔离
- 工作流编排 (多步骤 Prompt + 工具序列)

#### 8.4.2 核心抽象/接口

```typescript
// src/agent/skills/skills-runtime.ts
export interface SkillsRuntime {
  invoke(skillId: string, args: SkillArgs): Promise<SkillResult>;
  register(skill: Skill): void;
  unregister(id: string): void;
  list(): SkillMeta[];
  matchIntent(input: string): SkillMatch[];
  validate(skill: Skill): ValidationResult;
}

export interface Skill {
  meta: SkillMeta;
  promptTemplate: string;             // Handlebars 模板
  toolWhitelist: string[];            // 允许调用的工具 ID
  workflow?: SkillStep[];             // 工作流步骤
  preconditions?: SkillCondition[];   // 前置条件 (如需要某 MCP)
  variables: SkillVariable[];         // 可注入变量
}

export interface SkillMeta {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  description: string;
  version: string;
  category: SkillCategory;            // tdd | refactor | review | scaffold | docs | migration | security
  tags: string[];
  author?: string;
  intentPatterns: string[];           // 意图匹配正则/关键词
  riskLevel: RiskLevel;
  enabled: boolean;
}

export interface SkillStep {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  promptTemplate: string;
  toolCalls?: ToolCallTemplate[];
  condition?: string;                 // 条件执行
  onResult?: 'continue' | 'pause' | 'branch';
}

export interface SkillMatch {
  skillId: string;
  confidence: number;                 // 0-1
  matchedPattern: string;
  suggestedArgs?: SkillArgs;
}

export interface SkillResult {
  skillId: string;
  success: boolean;
  outputs: Record<string, unknown>;
  toolCalls: ToolCall[];
  nextSuggestedSkill?: string;
}
```

#### 8.4.3 Skill YAML 示例

```yaml
# .OpenWork/skills/tdd-feature.yaml
meta:
  id: tdd-feature
  name: TDD Feature Development
  description: 测试驱动开发新功能
  version: 1.0.0
  category: tdd
  tags: [testing, development]
  intentPatterns:
    - "写.*测试"
    - "TDD"
    - "测试驱动"
  riskLevel: L1
  enabled: true

promptTemplate: |
  你是 TDD 专家。请按以下步骤为 {{feature_name}} 实现:
  1. 先写失败的测试
  2. 实现最小代码使测试通过
  3. 重构
  上下文: {{context}}

toolWhitelist:
  - read_file
  - write_file
  - edit_file
  - run_command
  - grep
  - glob

workflow:
  - id: write_test
    name: 编写失败测试
    promptTemplate: "为 {{feature_name}} 编写测试用例"
  - id: run_test_red
    name: 运行测试 (应失败)
    toolCalls:
      - tool: run_command
        args: { command: "npm test" }
    onResult: continue
  - id: implement
    name: 实现最小代码
    promptTemplate: "实现 {{feature_name}} 使测试通过"
  - id: run_test_green
    name: 运行测试 (应通过)
    toolCalls:
      - tool: run_command
        args: { command: "npm test" }
  - id: refactor
    name: 重构
    promptTemplate: "重构代码，保持测试通过"
    condition: "test_passed == true"

variables:
  - name: feature_name
    type: string
    required: true
  - name: context
    type: string
    default: ""
```

#### 8.4.4 与其他模块的交互

- **Loop Engine**: Skill 可由 Loop 自动触发 (基于意图匹配) 或用户显式调用 `/skill tdd-feature`
- **Tool Use Layer**: Skill 限制只能调用 `toolWhitelist` 内的工具
- **Prompt Engine**: Skill 的 `promptTemplate` 作为 L5 Task Template 注入
- **Plugin System**: 插件可贡献 Skills (通过 `contributes.skills`)
- **Permission Gateway**: Skill 内的工具调用仍需经过权限网关

#### 8.4.5 关键设计决策

| ID | 决策 | 理由 |
|----|------|------|
| D6.2.1 | Skill 用 YAML 而非代码 | 降低门槛，非开发者也可创建 |
| D6.2.2 | 工具白名单隔离 | 防止 Skill 越权调用 |
| D6.2.3 | 意图匹配用正则+关键词 (非 LLM) | 节省 token，快速匹配 |
| D6.2.4 | 工作流支持条件分支 | 复杂场景需要分支逻辑 |

#### 8.4.6 配置项

```yaml
skills:
  builtin_enabled: true
  disabled: []                     # 禁用的 skill id
  custom_dir: ".OpenWork/skills"
  auto_match: true                 # 自动匹配意图
  match_confidence_threshold: 0.7
  marketplace:
    enabled: true
    registry: "https://registry.openwork.dev/skills"
```

---

### 8.5 模块 3: Tool Use Layer

#### 8.5.1 模块定位与职责

Tool Use Layer 是 Harness 的执行骨架。它定义工具如何注册、调度、沙箱、审计。

**核心职责**:
- 18+ 内置工具 (文件/搜索/命令/编辑器/浏览器/Web/Memory/Todo/Task/MCP/Skill)
- 工具注册与发现 (声明式 schema)
- 工具调度 (同步/流式/并发/后台)
- 沙箱执行 (路径白名单 / 资源限制)
- 审计日志 (append-only)

#### 8.5.2 核心抽象/接口

```typescript
// src/agent/tools/tool-registry.ts
export interface Tool {
  meta: ToolMeta;
  invoke(args: unknown, ctx: ToolContext): Promise<ToolResult>;
  validateArgs(args: unknown): ValidationResult;
}

export interface ToolMeta {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  description: string;
  parameters: JsonSchema;           // JSON Schema 入参定义
  returns?: JsonSchema;             // 出参定义 (可选)
  riskLevel: RiskLevel;             // L0-L3
  requiresApproval: boolean;
  defaultTimeoutMs: number;
  isStreaming: boolean;
  category: ToolCategory;
  source: ToolSource;               // builtin | mcp | plugin | skill | custom
  sourceId?: string;                // MCP server_id / plugin_id / skill_id
  version: string;
}

export type RiskLevel = 'L0' | 'L1' | 'L2' | 'L3';
export type ToolCategory =
  | 'file' | 'search' | 'command' | 'editor' | 'browser'
  | 'web' | 'memory' | 'todo' | 'task' | 'mcp' | 'skill';
export type ToolSource = 'builtin' | 'mcp' | 'plugin' | 'skill' | 'custom';

export interface ToolContext {
  sessionId: string;
  projectId: string;
  workingDir: string;
  permissions: PermissionSet;
  timeout: number;
  cancellationToken: CancellationToken;
}

export interface ToolResult {
  callId: string;
  success: boolean;
  output: unknown;
  error?: ToolError;
  durationMs: number;
  tokensConsumed?: number;
}

export interface ToolRegistry {
  register(tool: Tool): Promise<void>;
  unregister(toolId: string): Promise<void>;
  get(toolId: string): Tool | undefined;
  list(): ToolMeta[];
  listBySource(source: ToolSource): ToolMeta[];
  listByCategory(category: ToolCategory): ToolMeta[];
  search(query: string): ToolMeta[];
}

export interface ToolDispatcher {
  dispatch(call: ToolCall, ctx: ToolContext): Promise<ToolResult>;
  dispatchBatch(calls: ToolCall[], ctx: ToolContext): Promise<ToolResult[]>;
  dispatchStream(call: ToolCall, ctx: ToolContext): AsyncIterable<ToolChunk>;
}
```

#### 8.5.3 内置工具清单 (18+)

| 工具 | 类别 | 风险 | 说明 |
|------|------|:---:|------|
| `read_file` | file | L0 | 读取文件 (含行范围) |
| `write_file` | file | L1 | 写文件 (含新建/覆盖) |
| `edit_file` | file | L1 | 精确字符串替换 / 多点编辑 (9 层匹配策略) |
| `delete_file` | file | L2 | 删除文件 |
| `move_file` | file | L1 | 移动/重命名 |
| `glob` | search | L0 | 文件名模式匹配 |
| `grep` | search | L0 | 内容正则搜索 (ripgrep 内核) |
| `search_codebase` | search | L0 | 语义搜索 (向量+FTS+符号) |
| `run_command` | command | L2 | 执行终端命令 |
| `run_background` | command | L1 | 后台长任务 (dev server 等) |
| `editor_action` | editor | L0 | 光标移动/折叠/格式化 |
| `diagnostics` | editor | L0 | 获取 LSP 诊断 |
| `browser_navigate` | browser | L1 | 浏览器导航 |
| `browser_click` | browser | L1 | 点击元素 |
| `browser_screenshot` | browser | L0 | 截图 |
| `web_search` | web | L0 | 联网搜索 |
| `web_fetch` | web | L0 | 抓取网页为 markdown |
| `memory_write` | memory | L0 | 写入记忆 |
| `memory_read` | memory | L0 | 读取记忆 |
| `todo_write` | todo | L0 | 任务清单管理 |
| `task_spawn` | task | L1 | 派生 Sub-Agent |
| `mcp_call` | mcp | inherit | 调用 MCP 工具 |
| `skill_invoke` | skill | inherit | 调用 Skill |

#### 8.5.4 工具调用流程

```
LLM 请求调用 tool_X(args)
   │
   ▼
[Hooks Runtime] pre_act 钩子 (可拦截/改写)
   │
   ▼
[Permission Gateway] 校验权限 (L0 自动 / L1 配置 / L2 确认 / L3 强制确认)
   │
   ├─ 拒绝 → 返回结构化错误给 LLM
   │
   ▼
[Tool Dispatcher] 路由到对应 Worker
   │
   ▼
[Sandbox] 在受限环境执行
   ─ 文件操作: 路径白名单 (项目根 + allowlist)
   ─ 命令执行: allowlist + blocklist 双重过滤
   ─ 网络访问: 域名白名单 (可选)
   ─ 资源限制: CPU / 内存 / 时间
   │
   ▼
[Audit Logger] 记录调用 + 入参 + 出参 + 耗时 + 结果 + 批准者
   │
   ▼
[Hooks Runtime] on_tool_result 钩子
   │
   ▼
回流到 Loop (作为 observation)
```

#### 8.5.5 9 层编辑匹配策略 (edit_file)

参考 OpenCode 的 9 层容错编辑匹配，避免 LLM 输出微小差异导致编辑失败:

```typescript
// src/agent/tools/edit-file.ts
export class EditFileTool implements Tool {
  meta: ToolMeta = { /* ... */ };

  async invoke(args: EditArgs, ctx: ToolContext): Promise<ToolResult> {
    const matchers: EditMatcher[] = [
      new ExactMatch(),          // 1. 精确匹配
      new WhitespaceNormalize(), // 2. 空白归一化
      new LineNumberMatch(),     // 3. 行号匹配
      new FuzzyMatch(0.95),      // 4. 模糊匹配 (高阈值)
      new FuzzyMatch(0.85),      // 5. 模糊匹配 (中阈值)
      new FuzzyMatch(0.70),      // 6. 模糊匹配 (低阈值)
      new ContextAnchorMatch(),  // 7. 上下文锚点匹配
      new AstNodeMatch(),        // 8. AST 节点匹配 (tree-sitter)
      new RegexMatch(),          // 9. 正则回退
    ];

    for (const matcher of matchers) {
      const result = await matcher.match(args, ctx);
      if (result.matched) {
        return this.applyEdit(result, ctx);
      }
    }
    return { success: false, error: { code: 'NO_MATCH', message: '所有匹配策略失败' } };
  }
}
```

#### 8.5.6 关键设计决策

| ID | 决策 | 理由 |
|----|------|------|
| D6.3.1 | 工具与 LLM 解耦，经权限网关 | 安全可控，可审计 |
| D6.3.2 | 工具失败返回结构化错误而非中断 | 让 Agent 自我修复 |
| D6.3.3 | 9 层编辑匹配策略 | LLM 输出非精确，需容错 |
| D6.3.4 | 模型感知的工具筛选 | GPT → apply_patch, Claude → edit |
| D6.3.5 | 审计日志 append-only + 可选签名 | 合规与可复现 |

#### 8.5.7 配置项

```yaml
tools:
  builtin_enabled: true
  disabled: []
  edit_file:
    matchers: [exact, whitespace, line_number, fuzzy_high, fuzzy_mid, fuzzy_low, anchor, ast, regex]
  dispatcher:
    concurrency: 4
    default_timeout: 60s
    interruptible: true
  sandbox:
    file_scope: "project+allowlist"
    command:
      allowlist: []
      blocklist: ["rm -rf /", "sudo", "git push --force", "> /dev/"]
    network_whitelist: null
```

### 8.6 模块 4: Context Engine

#### 8.6.1 模块定位与职责

Context Engine 决定「Agent 在每一次推理时，知道什么、忘记什么、如何检索、如何压缩」。它直接决定输出质量与成本。

**核心职责**:
- 10 类上下文来源采集
- 分层压缩 (近因/摘要/事实/代码指纹)
- Hybrid RAG (向量 + BM25 + 结构)
- Token 窗口管理 (优先级队列 + 钉选 + 驱逐)

#### 8.6.2 核心抽象/接口

```typescript
// src/agent/context/context-engine.ts
export interface ContextEngine {
  collect(sources: ContextSource[]): Promise<RawContext>;
  compress(history: Message[], budget: TokenBudget): Promise<CompressedContext>;
  retrieve(query: string, topK: number): Promise<RetrievalResult[]>;
  manageWindow(items: ContextItem[]): ContextWindow;
  pin(itemId: string): void;
  unpin(itemId: string): void;
  getEvictionLog(): EvictionRecord[];
}

export type ContextSource =
  | 'explicit'         // 用户选中代码 / @文件 / @符号
  | 'file'             // 当前文件、相关文件
  | 'repo_index'       // 全仓库符号、依赖图 (RAG 召回)
  | 'git'              // diff / log / branch / blame
  | 'terminal'         // 最近命令、输出 (白名单)
  | 'editor'           // 光标、诊断 (LSP)、折叠区
  | 'browser'          // 文档页 / 控制台 / 网络 (可选)
  | 'project_config'   // package.json / pyproject / tsconfig
  | 'session_history'  // 当前会话 + 跨会话记忆
  | 'external_knowledge'; // 文档 RAG / Web

export interface TokenBudget {
  total: number;                    // model.context_window
  reserveOutput: number;            // 输出预留
  reserveTools: number;             // 工具声明预留
  reserveDynamic: number;           // 动态上下文预留
  perSource: Map<ContextSource, number>;
}

export interface CompressedContext {
  recencyWindow: Message[];         // 最近 N 轮原样
  summaryWindow: Summary[];         // 较早的按 K 轮摘要
  factsWindow: Fact[];              // 抽取的结构化事实
  codeFingerprints: CodeFingerprint[]; // 已读文件 hash + 摘要
  totalTokens: number;
}

export interface RetrievalResult {
  content: string;
  source: string;
  score: number;
  metadata: RetrievalMetadata;
}

export interface ContextWindow {
  items: ContextItem[];
  totalTokens: number;
  pinned: Set<string>;
  evicted: EvictionRecord[];
}

export interface ContextItem {
  id: string;
  source: ContextSource;
  content: string;
  tokens: number;
  weight: number;                   // 重要度 (相关性 × 时效性 × 用户标记)
  pinned: boolean;
  createdAt: number;
}
```

#### 8.6.3 分层压缩策略

```typescript
export type CompressionStrategy =
  | { type: 'compress_dynamic'; recencyWindow: number; summaryChunk: number; extractFacts: boolean }
  | { type: 'truncate_tail'; maxTokens: number }
  | { type: 'summarize_all' }
  | { type: 'drop_low_weight'; keepRatio: number };

export class ContextCompressor {
  async compress(history: Message[], budget: TokenBudget): Promise<CompressedContext> {
    const recency = history.slice(-this.config.recencyWindow);
    const older = history.slice(0, -this.config.recencyWindow);

    // 分块摘要
    const chunks = this.chunkBy(older, this.config.summaryChunk);
    const summaries = await Promise.all(
      chunks.map(c => this.summarize(c)),
    );

    // 事实抽取
    const facts = this.config.extractFacts
      ? await this.extractFacts(summaries)
      : [];

    // 代码指纹
    const fingerprints = await this.computeFingerprints(history);

    return {
      recencyWindow: recency,
      summaryWindow: summaries,
      factsWindow: facts,
      codeFingerprints: fingerprints,
      totalTokens: this.countTokens({ recency, summaries, facts, fingerprints }),
    };
  }
}
```

#### 8.6.4 Hybrid RAG 检索

```typescript
export class HybridRetriever {
  constructor(
    private vectorIndex: VectorIndex,    // sqlite-vec / LanceDB
    private ftsIndex: FtsIndex,          // SQLite FTS5
    private symbolIndex: SymbolIndex,    // tree-sitter 符号图
  ) {}

  async retrieve(query: string, topK: number = 10): Promise<RetrievalResult[]> {
    // 三路并行检索
    const [vectorResults, ftsResults, symbolResults] = await Promise.all([
      this.vectorIndex.search(query, topK * 2),
      this.ftsIndex.search(query, topK * 2),
      this.symbolIndex.search(query, topK),
    ]);

    // RRF (Reciprocal Rank Fusion) 融合
    const fused = this.rrfFusion([
      { results: vectorResults, weight: 1.0 },
      { results: ftsResults, weight: 1.0 },
      { results: symbolResults, weight: 1.2 },  // 结构信号加权
    ]);

    return fused.slice(0, topK);
  }

  private rrfFusion(paths: { results: RetrievalResult[]; weight: number }[]): RetrievalResult[] {
    const rrfK = 60;
    const scores = new Map<string, number>();
    for (const { results, weight } of paths) {
      results.forEach((r, i) => {
        const score = weight / (rrfK + i + 1);
        scores.set(r.content, (scores.get(r.content) ?? 0) + score);
      });
    }
    return [...scores.entries()]
      .map(([content, score]) => ({ content, score, source: 'hybrid', metadata: {} }))
      .sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);
  }
}
```

#### 8.6.5 关键设计决策

| ID | 决策 | 理由 |
|----|------|------|
| D6.4.1 | 分层摘要而非全文截断 | 保留长期事实，降低信息丢失 |
| D6.4.2 | 默认混合检索 (向量+BM25+结构) | 单一检索召回不足，代码场景结构信号强 |
| D6.4.3 | 索引本地优先，向量库可换 | 兼顾隐私与扩展 |
| D6.4.4 | 上下文驱逐可回溯 | 避免黑盒，便于调试 |
| D6.4.5 | 关键信息保全: 压缩前后做 diff 校验 | 摘要丢失关键事实时告警 |

#### 8.6.6 配置项

```yaml
context:
  sources:
    explicit: { weight: 1.0, auto_pin: true }
    file:    { weight: 0.9 }
    repo_index: { weight: 0.6 }
    git:     { weight: 0.7 }
    terminal: { weight: 0.5, whitelist: ["git", "npm", "cargo"] }
    editor:  { weight: 0.7 }
    browser: { weight: 0.3, enabled: false }
    project_config: { weight: 0.6 }
    session_history: { weight: 1.0 }
    external_knowledge: { weight: 0.3 }
  compression:
    strategy: "compress_dynamic"
    recency_window: 6
    summary_chunk: 4
    facts_extraction: true
  budget:
    policy: "model_aware"
    reserve_output: 4096
    reserve_tools: 2048
  retrieval:
    vector_backend: "embedded"  # embedded | lancedb | qdrant
    hybrid: true
    top_k: 10
    rrf_k: 60
  eviction:
    policy: "lru_importance"
    log_evicted: true
```

### 8.7 模块 5: Memory System

#### 8.7.1 模块定位与职责

Memory System 实现三级记忆，让 Agent 跨会话、跨项目积累经验。

**核心职责**:
- 工作记忆 (会话级，可存档)
- 项目记忆 (持久，类似 `AGENTS.md` 增强版)
- 全局记忆 (跨项目，用户偏好与通用经验)
- 向量索引 (语义检索记忆)
- 记忆遗忘与合并 (consolidation)

#### 8.7.2 核心抽象/接口

```typescript
// src/agent/memory/memory-system.ts
export interface MemorySystem {
  write(scope: MemoryScope, entry: MemoryEntry): Promise<MemoryId>;
  read(id: MemoryId): Promise<MemoryEntry | null>;
  search(query: MemoryQuery): Promise<MemoryEntry[]>;
  update(id: MemoryId, patch: Partial<MemoryEntry>): Promise<void>;
  forget(id: MemoryId): Promise<void>;
  consolidate(scope: MemoryScope): Promise<ConsolidationResult>;
  export(scope: MemoryScope): Promise<MemoryExport>;
  import(data: MemoryExport): Promise<void>;
}

export type MemoryScope = 'work' | 'project' | 'global';

export interface MemoryEntry {
  id: string;
  scope: MemoryScope;
  category: MemoryCategory;          // decision | fact | preference | lesson | todo
  content: string;
  embedding?: Float32Array;          // 向量 (sqlite-vec)
  metadata: MemoryMetadata;
  createdAt: number;
  updatedAt: number;
  expiresAt?: number;                // 可选过期
  confidence: number;                // 0-1
  source: MemorySource;              // agent | user | hook | consolidation
}

export interface MemoryQuery {
  scope?: MemoryScope;
  category?: MemoryCategory;
  semantic?: string;                 // 语义检索
  keyword?: string;                  // 关键词
  timeRange?: { from: number; to: number };
  limit?: number;
}

export interface ConsolidationResult {
  merged: MemoryId[];
  created: MemoryId[];
  deleted: MemoryId[];
  summary: string;
}
```

#### 8.7.3 三级记忆存储位置

| 级别 | 范围 | 生命周期 | 存储位置 | 用途 |
|------|------|----------|----------|------|
| 工作记忆 | 当前会话 | 会话结束清空 (可存档) | SQLite `memories` 表 (scope='work') | 当前任务上下文 |
| 项目记忆 | 当前项目 | 持久 | SQLite + `.OpenWork/memory.jsonl` + `AGENTS.md` | 项目约定、决策、教训 |
| 全局记忆 | 跨项目 | 持久 | SQLite + `~/.OpenWork/memory/global.jsonl` | 用户偏好、通用经验 |

#### 8.7.4 记忆合并 (Consolidation)

```typescript
export class MemoryConsolidator {
  async consolidate(scope: MemoryScope): Promise<ConsolidationResult> {
    // 1. 收集所有记忆
    const memories = await this.db.memories.findByScope(scope);

    // 2. 聚类相似记忆
    const clusters = await this.clusterMemories(memories);

    // 3. 对每个聚类:
    //    - 合并重复 → 生成单一记忆
    //    - 抽取共性 → 生成抽象记忆
    //    - 删除冗余 → 标记 deleted
    const result: ConsolidationResult = { merged: [], created: [], deleted: [], summary: '' };
    for (const cluster of clusters) {
      if (cluster.length > 1) {
        const merged = await this.llmMerge(cluster);
        const newId = await this.memorySystem.write(scope, merged);
        for (const old of cluster) {
          await this.memorySystem.forget(old.id);
          result.deleted.push(old.id);
        }
        result.created.push(newId);
      }
    }
    return result;
  }
}
```

#### 8.7.5 与其他模块的交互

- **Context Engine**: 记忆作为 `session_history` 与 `external_knowledge` 来源注入上下文
- **Tool Use Layer**: `memory_write` / `memory_read` 工具暴露给 Agent
- **Hooks System**: `on_task_complete` 钩子自动提取决策写入项目记忆
- **Indexer Worker**: 记忆的向量索引由 Indexer 计算并维护

#### 8.7.6 配置项

```yaml
memory:
  work:
    type: "ephemeral_archivable"
    archive_on_session_end: false
  project:
    location: ".OpenWork/memory.jsonl"
    human_readable: "AGENTS.md"
    auto_extract_decisions: true
  global:
    location: "~/.OpenWork/memory/global.jsonl"
  consolidation:
    enabled: true
    interval: "weekly"            # daily | weekly | monthly
    similarity_threshold: 0.85
  embedding:
    model: "local"                # local | openai | none
    dimensions: 384
```

### 8.8 模块 6: Plugin System

#### 8.8.1 模块定位与职责

Plugin System 是生态护城河，允许第三方扩展 Agent 能力。

**核心职责**:
- 插件形态支持 (npm 包 / 本地目录 / WASM 模块)
- manifest 声明能力 (工具/命令/Skills/Hooks/UI/Persona)
- 沙箱隔离 (isolated-vm)
- 生命周期管理 (install → enable → configure → run → disable → uninstall)
- 插件市场 (索引/安装/更新/评分)

#### 8.8.2 核心抽象/接口

```typescript
// src/capabilities/plugin/plugin-manager.ts
export interface PluginManager {
  install(source: PluginSource): Promise<PluginId>;
  uninstall(id: PluginId): Promise<void>;
  enable(id: PluginId): Promise<void>;
  disable(id: PluginId): Promise<void>;
  configure(id: PluginId, config: unknown): Promise<void>;
  list(): PluginMeta[];
  get(id: PluginId): Plugin | null;
  invoke(id: PluginId, capability: string, args: unknown): Promise<unknown>;
}

export interface Plugin {
  meta: PluginMeta;
  manifest: PluginManifest;
  sandbox: PluginSandbox;
  status: PluginStatus;
}

export interface PluginManifest {
  name: string;
  version: string;
  engines: { openwork: string };
  permissions: PluginPermission[];
  contributes: PluginContributes;
  entry: string;                    // 入口文件
}

export interface PluginContributes {
  tools?: string[];                 // 工具定义文件
  commands?: string[];              // 命令定义文件
  skills?: string[];                // Skill 文件
  hooks?: Record<HookTrigger, string>;
  ui?: { panels?: string[]; settings?: string };
  persona?: string;
  interceptors?: string[];          // Loop 拦截器
}

export type PluginPermission =
  | { type: 'filesystem'; paths: string[] }
  | { type: 'network'; domains: string[] }
  | { type: 'ui'; components: string[] }
  | { type: 'memory'; scopes: MemoryScope[] }
  | { type: 'mcp'; servers: string[] };

export interface PluginSandbox {
  isolate: isolatedVM.Isolate;
  memoryLimit: number;              // 默认 64MB
  cpuLimit: number;                 // 默认 1s/调用
  permissions: PluginPermission[];
}

export type PluginStatus = 'installed' | 'enabled' | 'disabled' | 'error' | 'uninstalling';
```

#### 8.8.3 沙箱执行 (isolated-vm)

```typescript
// src/capabilities/plugin/plugin-sandbox.ts
import ivm from 'isolated-vm';

export class PluginSandboxImpl implements PluginSandbox {
  isolate: ivm.Isolate;
  context: ivm.Context;

  constructor(private manifest: PluginManifest) {
    this.isolate = new ivm.Isolate({ memoryLimit: 64 });
    this.context = this.isolate.createContextSync();
    this.setupSandbox();
  }

  private setupSandbox(): void {
    // 注入受限全局 API
    const jail = this.context.global;
    jail.setSync('global', jail.derefInto());

    // 仅暴露白名单 API
    jail.setSync('__opencode', {
      log: (...args: unknown[]) => this.log(args),
      registerTool: (def: unknown) => this.registerTool(def),
      callTool: (id: string, args: unknown) =>
        new ivm.ExternalCopy(this.callTool(id, args)).copyInto(),
    }, { copy: true });
  }

  async invoke(capatibility: string, args: unknown): Promise<unknown> {
    const fn = await this.context.global.get(capability);
    if (!fn || typeof fn !== 'function') {
      throw new Error(`Capability ${capability} not found`);
    }
    return fn.apply(undefined, [args], {
      timeout: 1000,                 // CPU 限制 1s
      arguments: { copy: true },
      result: { copy: true, promise: true },
    });
  }
}
```

#### 8.8.4 manifest 示例

```json
{
  "name": "openwork-jira",
  "version": "1.0.0",
  "engines": { "openwork": "^1.0" },
  "permissions": [
    { "type": "network", "domains": ["https://*.atlassian.net"] },
    { "type": "ui", "components": ["panel"] }
  ],
  "contributes": {
    "tools": ["./tools/issue.js"],
    "commands": ["./commands/create-issue.js"],
    "hooks": { "on_task_complete": "./hooks/log-to-jira.js" },
    "ui": { "panels": ["./ui/jira-panel.js"] }
  },
  "entry": "./index.js"
}
```

#### 8.8.5 关键设计决策

| ID | 决策 | 理由 |
|----|------|------|
| D6.6.1 | 插件用 isolated-vm 沙箱 | V8 隔离，比 Worker Thread 更轻量，权限粒度细 |
| D6.6.2 | manifest 声明权限，用户批准 | 安全可控 |
| D6.6.3 | 支持 npm 包/本地/WASM 三种形态 | 兼顾易用与性能 |
| D6.6.4 | 内存 64MB / CPU 1s 限制 | 防止恶意插件耗尽资源 |

#### 8.8.6 配置项

```yaml
plugins:
  builtin_enabled: true
  disabled: []
  sandbox:
    memory_limit_mb: 64
    cpu_limit_ms: 1000
    network_whitelist: []            # 全局网络白名单
  marketplace:
    enabled: true
    registry: "https://registry.openwork.dev/plugins"
    auto_update: false
```

### 8.9 模块 7: MCP Integration

#### 8.9.1 模块定位与职责

MCP (Model Context Protocol) 是扩展工具/资源的一等公民协议。

**核心职责**:
- 支持 stdio / SSE / HTTP 三种 transport
- MCP Server 生命周期管理 (启动/停止/重启/健康检查)
- 工具/资源/Prompts 合并到统一 Registry
- 配置存储于 Profile，可版本化
- 内置 MCP Server (filesystem / git / fetch / sqlite)

#### 8.9.2 核心抽象/接口

```typescript
// src/capabilities/mcp/mcp-client.ts
export interface McpClient {
  connect(serverId: string): Promise<void>;
  disconnect(serverId: string): Promise<void>;
  listServers(): McpServerInfo[];
  listTools(serverId: string): Promise<ToolMeta[]>;
  listResources(serverId: string): Promise<McpResource[]>;
  listPrompts(serverId: string): Promise<McpPrompt[]>;
  callTool(serverId: string, toolName: string, args: unknown): Promise<ToolResult>;
  readResource(serverId: string, uri: string): Promise<unknown>;
  healthCheck(serverId: string): Promise<boolean>;
  onStatusChange(cb: (serverId: string, status: McpServerStatus) => void): void;
}

export interface McpServerConfig {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  transport: 'stdio' | 'sse' | 'http';
  // stdio
  command?: string;
  args?: string[];
  env?: Record<string, string>;
  // sse / http
  url?: string;
  auth?: McpAuth;
  enabled: boolean;
  autoRestart: boolean;
  healthCheckInterval: number;       // 默认 30s
}

export type McpServerStatus = 'connected' | 'disconnected' | 'error' | 'reconnecting';

export interface McpResource {
  uri: string;
  name: string;
  description?: string;
  mimeType?: string;
}

export interface McpPrompt {
  name: string;
  description?: string;
  arguments?: McpPromptArgument[];
}
```

#### 8.9.3 Transport 实现

```typescript
// src/capabilities/mcp/transports/stdio-transport.ts
import { spawn, ChildProcess } from 'node:child_process';
import { McpTransport } from './transport';

export class StdioTransport implements McpTransport {
  private process: ChildProcess | null = null;

  constructor(private config: { command: string; args: string[]; env?: Record<string, string> }) {}

  async connect(): Promise<void> {
    this.process = spawn(this.config.command, this.config.args, {
      env: { ...process.env, ...this.config.env },
      stdio: ['pipe', 'pipe', 'pipe'],
    });

    this.process.on('exit', (code) => {
      if (code !== 0) this.onDisconnect?.(code);
    });
    this.process.on('error', (err) => this.onError?.(err));
  }

  async send(message: McpMessage): Promise<void> {
    if (!this.process?.stdin) throw new Error('Not connected');
    this.process.stdin.write(JSON.stringify(message) + '\n');
  }

  onMessage(cb: (msg: McpMessage) => void): void {
    let buffer = '';
    this.process?.stdout?.on('data', (chunk) => {
      buffer += chunk.toString();
      const lines = buffer.split('\n');
      buffer = lines.pop() ?? '';
      for (const line of lines) {
        if (line.trim()) cb(JSON.parse(line));
      }
    });
  }

  async disconnect(): Promise<void> {
    this.process?.kill();
    this.process = null;
  }
}
```

#### 8.9.4 工具合并到统一 Registry

```typescript
// src/capabilities/mcp/mcp-registry-bridge.ts
export class McpRegistryBridge {
  constructor(private mcp: McpClient, private registry: ToolRegistry) {}

  async syncServers(): Promise<void> {
    const servers = this.mcp.listServers();
    for (const server of servers) {
      if (server.status !== 'connected') continue;
      const tools = await this.mcp.listTools(server.id);
      for (const tool of tools) {
        // 包装为统一 Tool 接口
        const wrappedTool = new McpToolWrapper(tool, server.id, this.mcp);
        await this.registry.register(wrappedTool);
      }
    }
  }
}

class McpToolWrapper implements Tool {
  meta: ToolMeta;

  constructor(
    private toolMeta: ToolMeta,
    private serverId: string,
    private mcp: McpClient,
  ) {
    this.meta = {
      ...toolMeta,
      source: 'mcp',
      sourceId: serverId,
      requiresApproval: toolMeta.riskLevel !== 'L0',
    };
  }

  async invoke(args: unknown, ctx: ToolContext): Promise<ToolResult> {
    const result = await this.mcp.callTool(this.serverId, this.meta.name, args);
    return result;
  }
}
```

### 8.10 模块 8: Hooks System

#### 8.10.1 模块定位与职责

Hooks System 在 Agent 生命周期的固定点触发用户脚本，可拦截/改写行为。

**核心职责**:
- 13+ Hook 点覆盖全生命周期
- 可拦截/改写 (pre_act 阻止工具调用 / post_observe 改写观察)
- 沙箱执行 (isolated-vm)
- 用户脚本 (TS/JS) + 内置钩子

#### 8.10.2 核心抽象/接口

```typescript
// src/agent/hooks/hooks-runtime.ts
export interface HooksRuntime {
  execute(trigger: HookTrigger, ctx: HookContext): Promise<HookResult>;
  register(hook: HookConfig): void;
  unregister(id: string): void;
  list(filter?: HookFilter): HookConfig[];
}

export type HookTrigger =
  | 'on_session_start'
  | 'on_session_end'
  | 'pre_plan'
  | 'post_plan'
  | 'pre_act'                 // 可拦截/改写工具调用
  | 'post_observe'            // 可改写 observation
  | 'on_tool_call'
  | 'on_tool_result'
  | 'on_file_change'
  | 'on_test_fail'
  | 'on_task_complete'
  | 'pre_model_call'          // 可改写 prompt
  | 'post_model_call'         // 可改写 LLM 输出
  | 'on_cost_warning';

export interface HookConfig {
  id: string;
  trigger: HookTrigger;
  script: string;                   // 脚本路径或内联代码
  priority: number;                 // 执行优先级 (低→高)
  enabled: boolean;
  async: boolean;                   // 是否异步执行 (不阻塞 Loop)
  timeout: number;                  // 超时 (ms)
  conditions?: string;              // 条件表达式
}

export interface HookContext {
  sessionId: string;
  step?: number;
  phase?: LoopPhase;
  toolCall?: ToolCall;
  toolResult?: ToolResult;
  prompt?: Prompt;
  modelResult?: ModelResult;
  fileChange?: FileChange;
  costInfo?: CostInfo;
}

export interface HookResult {
  action: HookAction;
  modifiedData?: unknown;           // 改写后的数据
  injectedContext?: ContextItem[];  // 注入额外上下文
  reason?: string;
  error?: string;
}

export type HookAction =
  | 'continue'                      // 继续执行
  | 'block'                         // 阻止操作
  | 'modify'                        // 改写数据
  | 'inject'                        // 注入上下文
  | 'pause_loop';                   // 暂停循环
```

#### 8.10.3 Hook 执行流程

```typescript
export class HooksRuntimeImpl implements HooksRuntime {
  private hooks = new Map<HookTrigger, HookConfig[]>();

  async execute(trigger: HookTrigger, ctx: HookContext): Promise<HookResult> {
    const hooks = this.hooks.get(trigger) ?? [];
    if (hooks.length === 0) return { action: 'continue' };

    // 按 priority 排序
    const sorted = [...hooks].sort((a, b) => a.priority - b.priority);

    for (const hook of sorted) {
      if (!hook.enabled) continue;
      if (hook.conditions && !this.evaluateCondition(hook.conditions, ctx)) continue;

      const result = await this.executeWithTimeout(hook, ctx);
      if (result.action === 'block') {
        return { action: 'block', reason: result.reason };
      }
      if (result.action === 'modify' || result.action === 'inject') {
        // 合并改写
        Object.assign(ctx, result.modifiedData ?? {});
      }
      if (result.action === 'pause_loop') {
        return result;
      }
    }
    return { action: 'continue' };
  }

  private async executeWithTimeout(hook: HookConfig, ctx: HookContext): Promise<HookResult> {
    return new Promise((resolve) => {
      const timer = setTimeout(() => resolve({ action: 'continue', error: 'timeout' }), hook.timeout);
      this.sandbox.invoke(hook.script, ctx)
        .then((result) => { clearTimeout(timer); resolve(result); })
        .catch((err) => { clearTimeout(timer); resolve({ action: 'continue', error: err.message }); });
    });
  }
}
```

#### 8.10.4 典型 Hook 用例

```yaml
# .OpenWork/hooks.yaml
hooks:
  - id: lint-on-save
    trigger: on_file_change
    script: "./hooks/lint.js"
    conditions: "fileChange.extension in ['ts', 'tsx', 'js']"
    async: true
    timeout: 5000

  - id: block-force-push
    trigger: pre_act
    script: "./hooks/block-force-push.js"
    conditions: "toolCall.toolId == 'run_command' && toolCall.args.command contains 'push --force'"
    priority: 100

  - id: log-to-jira
    trigger: on_task_complete
    script: "./hooks/jira-log.js"
    async: true
    timeout: 10000

  - id: cost-warning-pause
    trigger: on_cost_warning
    script: "./hooks/cost-pause.js"
    priority: 50
```

```yaml

  builtin_enabled: true
  disabled: []
  custom_dir: ".OpenWork/hooks"
  default_timeout: 5000
  sandbox:
    memory_limit_mb: 32
    cpu_limit_ms: 1000
```

### 8.11 模块 9: Permission Gateway

#### 8.11.1 模块定位与职责

Permission Gateway 是所有工具执行的安全闸门。

**核心职责**:
- L0-L3 四级权限分级
- 审批缓存 (once / session / project / permanent)
- Dry-run 预览 (危险操作前显示 diff)
- 规则引擎 (allowlist + blocklist)
- 不可擦除审计日志

#### 8.11.2 核心抽象/接口

```typescript
// src/security/permission-gateway.ts
export interface PermissionGateway {
  check(request: PermissionRequest): Promise<PermissionDecision>;
  requestApproval(action: HitlAction): Promise<ApprovalResult>;
  cacheApproval(signature: string, scope: ApprovalScope): Promise<void>;
  checkCache(signature: string): Promise<ApprovalScope | null>;
  dryRun(call: ToolCall): Promise<DryRunResult>;
  listRules(): PermissionRule[];
  addRule(rule: PermissionRule): Promise<void>;
}

export interface PermissionRequest {
  toolId: string;
  toolMeta: ToolMeta;
  args: unknown;
  ctx: ToolContext;
  callId: string;
}

export interface PermissionDecision {
  allowed: boolean;
  reason: string;
  approvalType?: ApprovalType;
  dryRunPreview?: DryRunResult;
}

export type ApprovalType = 'auto' | 'cached' | 'user_approved' | 'denied';

export type ApprovalScope = 'once' | 'session' | 'project' | 'permanent';

export interface ApprovalResult {
  approved: boolean;
  scope: ApprovalScope;
  dryRunAccepted?: boolean;
}

export interface DryRunResult {
  summary: string;
  diff?: string;                    // 文件变更 diff
  affectedFiles?: string[];
  reversible: boolean;
}

export interface PermissionRule {
  id: string;
  toolId: string;                   // 工具 ID 或通配符
  pattern?: string;                 // 参数匹配模式 (正则)
  action: 'allow' | 'deny' | 'confirm';
  scope: ApprovalScope;
  conditions?: string;
}
```

#### 8.11.3 权限决策流水线

```
工具调用请求
  │
  ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                    Permission Gateway                            │
│                                                                  │
│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │
│  │                  Decision Pipeline                        │   │
│  │                                                           │   │
│  │  ① Risk Assessment ──► ② Rule Lookup ──► ③ Cache Check   │   │
│  │       │                      │                    │       │   │
│  │       ▼                      ▼                    ▼       │   │
│  │  L0: auto_allow        per_tool rules       once/session  │   │
│  │  L1: configurable      default policy        /project     │   │
│  │  L2: confirm           allowlist/blocklist   /permanent   │   │
│  │  L3: always_confirm    force_confirm                       │   │
│  │                                                           │   │
│  │  ④ Dry-run Preview (L2/L3) ──► ⑤ UI Prompt ──► ⑥ Audit   │   │
│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │
│                                                                  │
│  输出: PermissionDecision { allowed, reason, approval_type }     │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

#### 8.11.4 权限分级

| 级别 | 行为 | 示例工具 |
|------|------|----------|
| **L0 ReadOnly** | 自动允许 | read_file / glob / grep / search_codebase / diagnostics |
| **L1 Mutate** | 配置策略决定 (auto/approve/deny) | write_file / edit_file / move_file / run_background |
| **L2 Destructive** | 默认需确认 | delete_file / run_command |
| **L3 Critical** | 永远需确认 + 不可擦除审计 | rm -rf / force push / sudo / 不可逆操作 |

#### 8.11.5 命令执行双重过滤

```typescript
// src/security/command-filter.ts
export class CommandFilter {
  constructor(
    private allowlist: RegExp[],
    private blocklist: RegExp[],
  ) {}

  check(command: string): { allowed: boolean; reason?: string } {
    // 1. Blocklist 优先 (黑名单)
    for (const pattern of this.blocklist) {
      if (pattern.test(command)) {
        return { allowed: false, reason: `匹配 blocklist: ${pattern}` };
      }
    }
    // 2. Allowlist (白名单，若配置则强制)
    if (this.allowlist.length > 0) {
      const matched = this.allowlist.some(p => p.test(command));
      if (!matched) {
        return { allowed: false, reason: '不在 allowlist 中' };
      }
    }
    return { allowed: true };
  }
}

// 默认 blocklist
export const DEFAULT_BLOCKLIST = [
  /rm\s+-rf\s+\//,           // rm -rf /
  /sudo\s+/,                  // sudo
  /git\s+push\s+--force/,    // force push
  />\s*\/dev\//,              // 重定向到 /dev/
  /:\(\)\{\s*:\|:&\s*\};:/,  // fork bomb
  /mkfs/,                     // 格式化
  /dd\s+.*of=\/dev\//,       // dd 写设备
];
```

#### 8.11.6 配置项

```yaml
permission:
  default_policy: "approve"       # auto | approve | deny
  per_tool:
    run_command: "approve"
    delete_file: "always_confirm"
    write_file: "auto"
  approval_cache: session          # once | session | project | permanent
  dry_run:
    enabled: true
    on_level: ["L2", "L3"]
  audit:
    enabled: true
    append_only: true
    sign: false                    # 可选签名
    retention_days: 90
  rules:
    - tool_id: "run_command"
      pattern: "git push --force"
      action: deny
      scope: permanent
```
