### 8.12 模块 10: Subagent Manager

> **重点模块**：之前缺失严重，本节详细设计通信协议、并发控制、成本治理。

#### 8.12.1 模块定位与职责

Subagent Manager 让主 Agent 可派生隔离的子智能体处理子任务，实现专家分工、并行加速、上下文隔离。

**核心职责**:
- Persona 隔离 (每个 SubAgent 独立角色设定)
- 工具白名单 (限制 SubAgent 可调用工具)
- 上下文隔离 (SubAgent 不继承主 Agent 完整历史)
- 成本归属 (token/费用归属到父任务)
- 生命周期管理 (spawn → communicate → terminate)

#### 8.12.2 核心抽象/接口

```typescript
// src/agent/subagent/subagent-manager.ts
export interface SubAgentManager {
  spawn(config: SubAgentConfig, parentCtx: ParentContext): Promise<SubAgentHandle>;
  communicate(id: SubAgentId, message: AgentMessage): Promise<AgentMessage>;
  broadcast(message: AgentMessage, filter?: (id: SubAgentId) => boolean): Promise<void>;
  terminate(id: SubAgentId, reason?: string): Promise<TerminateResult>;
  listActive(): SubAgentHandle[];
  getCost(id: SubAgentId): SubAgentCost;
  getState(id: SubAgentId): SubAgentState;
  onEvent(id: SubAgentId, cb: (e: SubAgentEvent) => void): void;
}

export interface SubAgentConfig {
  id?: string;                      // 不提供则自动生成
  personaId: string;                // Persona 标识
  personaPrompt: string;            // Persona 提示词
  tools: string[];                  // 工具白名单
  skills?: string[];                // 可用 Skills
  model?: string;                   // 模型 (默认继承父 Agent)
  contextBudget: number;            // 上下文 token 预算
  costBudget: number;               // 成本预算 (USD)
  maxIterations: number;            // 最大迭代次数
  timeoutMs: number;                // 超时
  priority: 'high' | 'normal' | 'low';
  inheritMemory?: MemoryScope[];    // 继承的记忆范围
}

export interface SubAgentHandle {
  id: SubAgentId;
  config: SubAgentConfig;
  status: SubAgentStatus;
  createdAt: number;
  parentId?: SubAgentId;            // 支持嵌套
  messageQueue: AgentMessage[];
  cost: SubAgentCost;
}

export type SubAgentStatus =
  | 'spawning'
  | 'running'
  | 'waiting_for_input'
  | 'completed'
  | 'failed'
  | 'terminated'
  | 'timeout';

export interface SubAgentCost {
  tokensIn: number;
  tokensOut: number;
  costUsd: number;
  durationMs: number;
  toolCalls: number;
  iterationCount: number;
}

export interface AgentMessage {
  id: string;
  from: SubAgentId | 'main';
  to: SubAgentId | 'main';
  type: 'task' | 'result' | 'query' | 'response' | 'error' | 'progress';
  content: unknown;
  timestamp: number;
  replyTo?: string;                 // 回复的消息 ID
}

export interface ParentContext {
  sessionId: string;
  taskId: string;
  costBudgetRemaining: number;
  maxConcurrent: number;
}
```

#### 8.12.3 通信协议

SubAgent 与主 Agent 通过结构化 JSON 消息通信，**不直接共享上下文** (隔离原则)。

```typescript
// src/agent/subagent/subagent-protocol.ts
export interface SubAgentProtocol {
  /** 主 Agent 派发任务给 SubAgent */
  dispatchTask(handle: SubAgentHandle, task: SubTask): Promise<TaskResult>;

  /** SubAgent 向主 Agent 请求信息 (询问) */
  requestInfo(handle: SubAgentHandle, query: string): Promise<unknown>;

  /** SubAgent 上报进度 */
  reportProgress(handle: SubAgentHandle, progress: ProgressReport): Promise<void>;

  /** 主 Agent 中断 SubAgent */
  interrupt(handle: SubAgentHandle, reason: string): Promise<void>;

  /** SubAgent 完成任务，返回结果 */
  completeTask(handle: SubAgentHandle, result: TaskResult): Promise<void>;
}

export interface SubTask {
  id: string;
  description: string;
  inputs: Record<string, unknown>;
  expectedOutput: string;           // 期望输出描述
  deadline?: number;
}

export interface TaskResult {
  taskId: string;
  success: boolean;
  output: unknown;
  summary: string;                  // LLM 生成的摘要 (供主 Agent 理解)
  artifacts?: Artifact[];           // 产物 (文件/代码片段)
  lessonsLearned?: string[];        // 教训 (可写入记忆)
}

export interface ProgressReport {
  step: number;
  totalSteps?: number;
  phase: string;
  message: string;
}
```

#### 8.12.4 通信消息流示例

```
主 Agent                          SubAgent (reviewer)
   │                                    │
   │  dispatchTask(task="review file")  │
   ├───────────────────────────────────►│
   │                                    │
   │            requestInfo("read X")   │
   │◄───────────────────────────────────┤
   │                                    │
   │  response(fileContent)             │
   ├───────────────────────────────────►│
   │                                    │
   │       reportProgress("analyzing")  │
   │◄───────────────────────────────────┤
   │                                    │
   │       completeTask(result)         │
   │◄───────────────────────────────────┤
   │                                    │
   │  terminate("done")                 │
   ├───────────────────────────────────►│
```

#### 8.12.5 并发控制

```typescript
// src/agent/subagent/subagent-pool.ts
export class SubAgentPool {
  private active = new Map<SubAgentId, SubAgentHandle>();
  private queue: Array<{ config: SubAgentConfig; resolve: (h: SubAgentHandle) => void }> = [];

  constructor(
    private maxConcurrent: number = 4,
    private maxPerTask: number = 8,
    private costTracker: CostTracker,
  ) {}

  async acquire(config: SubAgentConfig, parentCtx: ParentContext): Promise<SubAgentHandle> {
    // 1. 检查并发上限
    if (this.active.size >= this.maxConcurrent) {
      await this.waitForSlot();
    }
    // 2. 检查每任务上限
    const perTaskCount = [...this.active.values()]
      .filter(h => h.config.id?.startsWith(parentCtx.taskId)).length;
    if (perTaskCount >= this.maxPerTask) {
      throw new Error(`每任务 SubAgent 上限 ${this.maxPerTask} 已达`);
    }
    // 3. 检查成本预算
    if (parentCtx.costBudgetRemaining < config.costBudget) {
      throw new Error(`父任务剩余预算不足: ${parentCtx.costBudgetRemaining} < ${config.costBudget}`);
    }
    // 4. Spawn
    const handle = await this.spawnSubAgent(config, parentCtx);
    this.active.set(handle.id, handle);
    return handle;
  }

  release(id: SubAgentId): void {
    this.active.delete(id);
    if (this.queue.length > 0) {
      const next = this.queue.shift()!;
      next.resolve(this.acquire(next.config, /* parentCtx */));
    }
  }

  private async waitForSlot(): Promise<void> {
    return new Promise((resolve) => {
      this.queue.push({ config: null as never, resolve: () => resolve() });
    });
  }
}
```

#### 8.12.6 成本治理

```typescript
// src/agent/subagent/cost-tracker.ts
export class CostTracker {
  private taskCosts = new Map<string, SubAgentCost>();
  private budgets = new Map<string, number>();

  /** 记录 SubAgent 成本 */
  record(subAgentId: SubAgentId, parentId: string | 'main', cost: Partial<SubAgentCost>): void {
    const current = this.taskCosts.get(subAgentId) ?? this.initCost();
    const updated: SubAgentCost = {
      tokensIn: current.tokensIn + (cost.tokensIn ?? 0),
      tokensOut: current.tokensOut + (cost.tokensOut ?? 0),
      costUsd: current.costUsd + (cost.costUsd ?? 0),
      durationMs: current.durationMs + (cost.durationMs ?? 0),
      toolCalls: current.toolCalls + (cost.toolCalls ?? 0),
      iterationCount: current.iterationCount + (cost.iterationCount ?? 0),
    };
    this.taskCosts.set(subAgentId, updated);

    // 父任务预算联动扣减
    if (parentId !== 'main') {
      const parentBudget = this.budgets.get(parentId);
      if (parentBudget !== undefined) {
        const newBudget = parentBudget - (cost.costUsd ?? 0);
        this.budgets.set(parentId, newBudget);
        if (newBudget <= 0) {
          this.emit('budget_exceeded', { taskId: parentId, subAgentId });
        }
      }
    }
  }

  /** 检查是否超预算 */
  checkBudget(taskId: string): { exceeded: boolean; remaining: number } {
    const budget = this.budgets.get(taskId);
    if (budget === undefined) return { exceeded: false, remaining: Infinity };
    return { exceeded: budget <= 0, remaining: budget };
  }

  /** 成本归因报告 */
  report(taskId: string): CostReport {
    const descendants = this.getDescendants(taskId);
    const total = descendants.reduce(
      (sum, id) => {
        const c = this.taskCosts.get(id);
        if (!c) return sum;
        return {
          tokensIn: sum.tokensIn + c.tokensIn,
          tokensOut: sum.tokensOut + c.tokensOut,
          costUsd: sum.costUsd + c.costUsd,
          durationMs: sum.durationMs + c.durationMs,
          toolCalls: sum.toolCalls + c.toolCalls,
        };
      },
      { tokensIn: 0, tokensOut: 0, costUsd: 0, durationMs: 0, toolCalls: 0 },
    );
    return { taskId, subagents: descendants, total };
  }
}
```

#### 8.12.7 上下文隔离策略

```typescript
export class SubAgentContextBuilder {
  /** 构建 SubAgent 的初始上下文 (不继承主 Agent 完整历史) */
  build(config: SubAgentConfig, parentCtx: ParentContext, task: SubTask): SubAgentContext {
    return {
      // 1. 任务描述 (主 Agent 显式传递)
      task,
      // 2. Persona (隔离)
      persona: config.personaPrompt,
      // 3. 工具清单 (白名单)
      tools: this.registry.listByWhitelist(config.tools),
      // 4. 继承的记忆 (按配置)
      memories: config.inheritMemory
        ? this.memory.search({ scope: config.inheritMemory, limit: 10 })
        : [],
      // 5. 项目上下文 (基础)
      projectContext: this.getProjectBasics(parentCtx),
      // 6. 不包含主 Agent 的会话历史 (隔离)
    };
  }
}
```

#### 8.12.8 关键设计决策

| ID | 决策 | 理由 |
|----|------|------|
| D6.10.1 | SubAgent 上下文隔离 | 防止上下文污染、降低成本 |
| D6.10.2 | 成本归属父任务 | 避免成本失控 |
| D6.10.3 | 并发上限 + 每任务上限 + 成本预算三重控制 | 防止 SubAgent 爆炸式烧 token |
| D6.10.4 | 通信用结构化 JSON 而非共享内存 | 隔离 + 可序列化 + 可审计 |
| D6.10.5 | 支持嵌套派生 (SubAgent 派生 SubAgent) | 复杂任务分解，但深度受限 (默认 2 层) |

#### 8.12.9 配置项

```yaml
sub_agents:
  enabled: true
  max_concurrent: 4                  # 全局并发上限
  max_per_task: 8                    # 每任务上限
  max_nesting_depth: 2               # 嵌套深度上限
  default:
    context_budget: 16000
    cost_budget: 0.50                # USD
    max_iterations: 15
    timeout_ms: 120000
  definitions:
    - id: "reviewer"
      persona: "strict senior code reviewer"
      tools: [read_file, grep, diagnostics]
      skills: ["web-design-guidelines"]
    - id: "test-writer"
      persona: "TDD practitioner"
      tools: [read_file, write_file, run_command, edit_file]
```

---

### 8.13 模块 11: Multi-agent Coordinator

> **重点模块**：之前缺失严重，本节详细设计协作模式、通信协议、并发控制、成本治理。

#### 8.13.1 模块定位与职责

Multi-agent Coordinator 在 Subagent Manager 之上提供多 Agent 协作编排能力，支持主从/并行/专家三种协作模式。

**核心职责**:
- 三种协作模式 (主从 / 并行 / 专家)
- 任务 DAG 编排 (拓扑排序 + 依赖管理)
- Agent 间通信协议 (发布订阅 + 直接寻址)
- 全局并发控制与资源调度
- 冲突检测与解决
- 全局成本治理

#### 8.13.2 核心抽象/接口

```typescript
// src/agent/coordinator/multi-agent-coordinator.ts
export interface MultiAgentCoordinator {
  /** 创建协作会话 */
  createSession(config: CollaborationConfig): Promise<CollaborationSession>;

  /** 派发任务到协作会话 */
  dispatchTask(sessionId: string, task: CollaborativeTask): Promise<CollaborativeResult>;

  /** 查询协作状态 */
  getStatus(sessionId: string): CollaborationStatus;

  /** 中断协作 */
  interrupt(sessionId: string, reason: string): Promise<void>;

  /** 获取协作报告 */
  getReport(sessionId: string): CollaborationReport;

  /** 订阅协作事件 */
  onEvent(sessionId: string, cb: (e: CollaborationEvent) => void): void;
}

export interface CollaborationConfig {
  mode: CollaborationMode;
  agents: AgentRole[];               // 参与角色
  taskDag?: TaskDag;                 // 任务 DAG (并行/专家模式)
  concurrencyLimit: number;
  globalCostBudget: number;
  communicationProtocol: 'pubsub' | 'direct' | 'shared_blackboard';
  conflictResolution: 'last_write_wins' | 'voting' | 'coordinator_decides' | 'human';
}

export type CollaborationMode =
  | 'master_slave'                   // 主从: 主 Agent 派发子任务
  | 'parallel'                       // 并行: 多 Agent 并行处理独立子任务
  | 'expert';                        // 专家: 不同 Agent 装载不同 Persona

export interface AgentRole {
  roleId: string;
  personaId: string;
  tools: string[];
  skills?: string[];
  expertise: string[];               // 专业领域
  model?: string;
}

export interface TaskDag {
  nodes: DagNode[];
  edges: DagEdge[];
}

export interface DagNode {
  id: string;
  task: SubTask;
  assignedTo?: string;               // 角色 ID
  status: 'pending' | 'running' | 'completed' | 'failed' | 'blocked';
  result?: TaskResult;
}

export interface DagEdge {
  from: string;
  to: string;
  type: 'dependency' | 'communication';
}

export interface CollaborationSession {
  id: string;
  config: CollaborationConfig;
  status: CollaborationStatus;
  createdAt: number;
  agents: Map<string, SubAgentHandle>;
  dag: TaskDag;
}

export interface CollaborationStatus {
  phase: 'planning' | 'executing' | 'reviewing' | 'completed' | 'failed' | 'interrupted';
  activeAgents: number;
  completedTasks: number;
  totalTasks: number;
  totalCost: number;
  conflictsDetected: number;
}

export interface CollaborationReport {
  sessionId: string;
  success: boolean;
  totalDuration: number;
  totalCost: number;
  agentReports: AgentReport[];
  conflictsResolved: number;
  lessonsLearned: string[];
}
```

#### 8.13.3 三种协作模式

##### A. 主从模式 (Master-Slave)

```
            ┌─────────────┐
            │ Master Agent│
            │  (协调者)    │
            └──────┬──────┘
                   │
        ┌──────────┼──────────┐
        │          │          │
        ▼          ▼          ▼
   ┌─────────┐┌─────────┐┌─────────┐
   │ Slave 1 ││ Slave 2 ││ Slave 3 │
   │ (任务A) ││ (任务B) ││ (任务C) │
   └─────────┘└─────────┘└─────────┘
```

- **场景**: 主 Agent 把大任务拆为子任务，派给 Slave 处理
- **通信**: Slave → Master (报告进度/请求信息)，Master → Slave (派发/中断)
- **优点**: 控制清晰，易于实现
- **缺点**: Master 是瓶颈

##### B. 并行模式 (Parallel)

```
   ┌──────────────────────────────────┐
   │       Coordinator (调度器)        │
   └──────────────────────────────────┘
            │
   ┌────────┼────────┬────────┐
   │        │        │        │
   ▼        ▼        ▼        ▼
┌─────┐ ┌─────┐ ┌─────┐ ┌─────┐
│Ag 1 │ │Ag 2 │ │Ag 3 │ │Ag 4 │   独立子任务并行
└──┬──┘ └──┬──┘ └──┬──┘ └──┬──┘
   │       │       │       │
   └───────┴───────┴───────┘
            │
            ▼
   ┌─────────────────┐
   │  结果聚合 (Aggregator) │
   └─────────────────┘
```

- **场景**: 多个独立子任务并行处理 (如读 5 个文件分别总结)
- **通信**: 通过 Shared Blackboard (共享黑板) 或 Aggregator
- **优点**: 速度最快
- **缺点**: 仅适合独立任务

##### C. 专家模式 (Expert)

```
            ┌─────────────────┐
            │  Coordinator    │
            │  (路由器)        │
            └────────┬────────┘
                     │
       ┌─────────────┼─────────────┐
       │             │             │
       ▼             ▼             ▼
┌──────────┐  ┌──────────┐  ┌──────────┐
│ Refactor │  │ Security │  │  Test    │
│ Expert   │  │ Expert   │  │  Expert  │
│ (重构)   │  │ (安全)   │  │  (测试)  │
└──────────┘  └──────────┘  └──────────┘
       │             │             │
       └─────────────┼─────────────┘
                     │
                     ▼
            ┌─────────────────┐
            │  Coordinator    │
            │  (结果合并)      │
            └─────────────────┘
```

- **场景**: 不同专家 Agent 处理不同维度的同一任务 (如代码评审 + 安全审计 + 测试覆盖)
- **通信**: Coordinator 路由 → 专家处理 → 结果合并
- **优点**: 专业深度
- **缺点**: 协调复杂

#### 8.13.4 通信协议

```typescript
// src/agent/coordinator/communication-protocol.ts
export interface CommunicationProtocol {
  /** 发布消息到主题 */
  publish(topic: string, message: CollaborationMessage): Promise<void>;

  /** 订阅主题 */
  subscribe(topic: string, subscriberId: string, cb: (msg: CollaborationMessage) => void): void;

  /** 直接发送消息 */
  sendDirect(from: string, to: string, message: CollaborationMessage): Promise<void>;

  /** 写入共享黑板 */
  writeToBlackboard(key: string, value: unknown, writer: string): Promise<void>;

  /** 读取共享黑板 */
  readFromBlackboard(key: string): Promise<BlackboardEntry | null>;

  /** 广播消息 */
  broadcast(message: CollaborationMessage, exclude?: string[]): Promise<void>;
}

export interface CollaborationMessage {
  id: string;
  from: string;
  to?: string | 'broadcast' | 'coordinator';
  type: CollaborationMessageType;
  payload: unknown;
  timestamp: number;
  priority: 'high' | 'normal' | 'low';
  requiresAck: boolean;
}

export type CollaborationMessageType =
  | 'task_assignment'
  | 'task_progress'
  | 'task_complete'
  | 'task_failed'
  | 'info_request'
  | 'info_response'
  | 'conflict_report'
  | 'resource_request'
  | 'resource_release'
  | 'coordination_directive';
```

#### 8.13.5 DAG 调度器

```typescript
// src/agent/coordinator/dag-scheduler.ts
export class DagScheduler {
  constructor(
    private dag: TaskDag,
    private pool: SubAgentPool,
    private protocol: CommunicationProtocol,
  ) {}

  async execute(): Promise<CollaborativeResult> {
    const completed = new Set<string>();
    const inProgress = new Set<string>();
    const failed = new Set<string>();

    while (completed.size + failed.size < this.dag.nodes.length) {
      // 1. 找出可执行节点 (依赖已满足)
      const ready = this.findReadyNodes(completed);
      if (ready.length === 0 && inProgress.size === 0) {
        // 死锁检测
        if (failed.size > 0) throw new Error('DAG 执行失败: 依赖无法满足');
        break;
      }

      // 2. 派发任务 (受并发限制)
      for (const node of ready) {
        if (inProgress.size >= this.config.concurrencyLimit) break;
        inProgress.add(node.id);
        this.dispatchNode(node).then((result) => {
          node.result = result;
          node.status = result.success ? 'completed' : 'failed';
          (result.success ? completed : failed).add(node.id);
          inProgress.delete(node.id);
        });
      }

      await this.sleep(100); // 避免忙等
    }
    return this.aggregateResults();
  }

  private findReadyNodes(completed: Set<string>): DagNode[] {
    return this.dag.nodes.filter((node) => {
      if (node.status !== 'pending') return false;
      // 检查所有依赖是否完成
      const deps = this.dag.edges
        .filter(e => e.to === node.id && e.type === 'dependency')
        .map(e => e.from);
      return deps.every(d => completed.has(d));
    });
  }
}
```

#### 8.13.6 冲突检测与解决

```typescript
// src/agent/coordinator/conflict-resolver.ts
export class ConflictResolver {
  constructor(private strategy: ConflictResolutionStrategy) {}

  async detect(operation: AgentOperation): Promise<Conflict | null> {
    // 检测文件冲突 (多 Agent 同时修改同一文件)
    if (operation.type === 'file_write') {
      const lock = await this.fileLocks.get(operation.path);
      if (lock && lock.holder !== operation.agentId) {
        return {
          type: 'file_conflict',
          resource: operation.path,
          holders: [lock.holder, operation.agentId],
        };
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  async resolve(conflict: Conflict): Promise<Resolution> {
    switch (this.strategy) {
      case 'last_write_wins':
        return { action: 'proceed', winner: conflict.holders[1] };

      case 'voting':
        // 各 Agent 投票决定
        const votes = await Promise.all(
          conflict.holders.map(id => this.requestVote(id, conflict)),
        );
        const winner = this.tallyVotes(votes);
        return { action: 'proceed', winner };

      case 'coordinator_decides':
        // Coordinator 决定
        const decision = await this.coordinatorDecide(conflict);
        return { action: decision.action, winner: decision.winner };

      case 'human':
        // 人工裁决
        const ruling = await this.requestHumanRuling(conflict);
        return { action: ruling.action, winner: ruling.winner };

      default:
        return { action: 'block', reason: 'unknown_strategy' };
    }
  }
}
```

#### 8.13.7 全局成本治理

```typescript
// src/agent/coordinator/global-cost-governor.ts
export class GlobalCostGovernor {
  private sessionBudgets = new Map<string, SessionBudget>();

  /** 检查协作会话是否超预算 */
  checkSession(sessionId: string): CostCheckResult {
    const budget = this.sessionBudgets.get(sessionId);
    if (!budget) return { exceeded: false, remaining: Infinity };

    const totalSpent = budget.spent;
    const remaining = budget.limit - totalSpent;

    return {
      exceeded: remaining <= 0,
      remaining,
      warning: remaining < budget.limit * 0.2, // 20% 预警
      perAgent: this.getPerAgentSpent(sessionId),
    };
  }

  /** 批准 SubAgent spawn (基于预算) */
  approveSpawn(sessionId: string, config: SubAgentConfig): ApprovalDecision {
    const check = this.checkSession(sessionId);
    if (check.exceeded) {
      return {
        approved: false,
        reason: 'session_budget_exceeded',
        remaining: check.remaining,
      };
    }
    // 单 Agent 预算检查
    if (config.costBudget && config.costBudget > check.remaining) {
      return {
        approved: false,
        reason: 'agent_budget_exceeds_remaining',
        requested: config.costBudget,
        remaining: check.remaining,
      };
    }
    // 预警但允许
    if (check.warning) {
      return {
        approved: true,
        warning: 'budget_low',
        remaining: check.remaining,
        recommendedLimit: check.remaining * 0.5,
      };
    }
    return { approved: true, remaining: check.remaining };
  }

  /** 记录 SubAgent 花费 (从父预算扣除) */
  recordSpend(sessionId: string, agentId: string, amount: number): void {
    const budget = this.sessionBudgets.get(sessionId);
    if (!budget) return;
    budget.spent += amount;
    budget.perAgent.set(agentId, (budget.perAgent.get(agentId) ?? 0) + amount);
    // 触发事件
    this.emit('spend', { sessionId, agentId, amount, remaining: budget.limit - budget.spent });
    if (budget.spent >= budget.limit) {
      this.emit('budget_exceeded', { sessionId, spent: budget.spent, limit: budget.limit });
    }
  }

  private getPerAgentSpent(sessionId: string): Record<string, number> {
    const budget = this.sessionBudgets.get(sessionId);
    if (!budget) return {};
    return Object.fromEntries(budget.perAgent);
  }
}

interface SessionBudget {
  limit: number;
  spent: number;
  perAgent: Map<string, number>;
}

interface CostCheckResult {
  exceeded: boolean;
  remaining: number;
  warning?: boolean;
  perAgent?: Record<string, number>;
}

interface ApprovalDecision {
  approved: boolean;
  reason?: string;
  remaining?: number;
  requested?: number;
  warning?: string;
  recommendedLimit?: number;
}
```

#### 8.13.8 关键设计决策

| 决策 ID | 决策 | 理由 | 备选方案 |
|---------|------|------|----------|
| **D6.13.1** | 三种协作模式而非自由编排 | 降低用户认知负担，覆盖 90% 场景 | 自由 DAG 编排 (复杂度过高) |
| **D6.13.2** | SubAgent 运行于独立 Worker | 隔离崩溃 + 并行执行 | 同 Worker 协程 (崩溃传染) |
| **D6.13.3** | DAG 调度器支持动态加边 | 运行时根据 LLM 输出新增依赖 | 静态 DAG (无法适配 LLM 动态规划) |
| **D6.13.4** | 冲突解决默认 `coordinator_decides` | 平衡自动化与正确性 | 默认 `human` (HITL 过重) |
| **D6.13.5** | 父子预算链式传递 | 防止 SubAgent 失控烧钱 | 全局预算 (粒度不足) |
| **D6.13.6** | `master_slave` 模式禁用 SubAgent 主动终止主 Agent | 防止权限反转 | 允许 (安全风险) |
| **D6.13.7** | `parallel` 模式结果合并采用 `merge_then_review` | 先合并再让主 Agent 复核 | 直接投票 (质量不可控) |
| **D6.13.8** | `expert` 模式 SubAgent 之间不通信 | 隔离专家视角，避免从众 | 允许通信 (易出现从众效应) |
| **D6.13.9** | 全局成本治理器独立于 Coordinator | 单一职责 + 可被任何模式复用 | 嵌入 Coordinator (耦合) |
| **D6.13.10** | 通信消息全部落库 | 审计 + 可观测 + 回放 | 内存通信 (无法回放) |

#### 8.13.9 配置项

```yaml
# config/multi-agent.yaml
coordinator:
  enabled: true
  default_mode: master_slave  # master_slave | parallel | expert
  max_concurrent_subagents: 4
  max_per_task_subagents: 8
  cost_budget_per_session: 5.0  # USD
  cost_budget_per_subagent: 1.0  # USD
  cost_warning_threshold: 0.2    # 20% 剩余时预警

  master_slave:
    allow_subagent_terminate_parent: false
    result_review_required: true

  parallel:
    merge_strategy: merge_then_review  # merge_then_review | vote | coordinator_picks
    vote_threshold: 0.5
    allow_partial_results: true
    partial_results_min_count: 2

  expert:
    allow_inter_agent_communication: false
    consensus_required: true
    consensus_threshold: 0.66  # 2/3 多数

  dag_scheduler:
    max_parallel_nodes: 4
    default_timeout_ms: 300000  # 5 分钟
    retry_on_failure: 2
    checkpoint_interval: 10      # 每 10 个节点存一次 checkpoint

  conflict_resolver:
    default_strategy: coordinator_decides
    file_write_strategy: last_writer_wins  # 文件写冲突策略
    autonomous_strategy: first_one_wins     # 自主操作冲突策略
    human_strategy: queue_and_wait          # 人工介入策略
    human_timeout_ms: 60000

  cost_governor:
    enforce_budget: true
    hard_stop_on_exceed: true
    warn_at_remaining_percent: 20
    record_spend_interval_ms: 1000
```

---

## 9. 能力扩展层架构

### 9.1 层级定位与职责

能力扩展层 (Layer 7) 是 Agent 与外部世界交互的「手脚」。它将 LLM 的抽象工具调用意图转化为具体的文件操作、Shell 命令、MCP 服务调用、插件执行、编辑器/终端/浏览器桥接等动作。所有执行均需经过权限网关 (L5) 仲裁。

```
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                  能力扩展层 (Capabilities) L4                  │
│                                                                │
│  ┌─────────────┐  ┌─────────────┐  ┌─────────────────────┐  │
│  │ Tool        │  │ MCP Client  │  │ Plugin Sandbox      │  │
│  │ Registry    │  │ (stdio/SSE/ │  │ (isolated-vm)       │  │
│  │             │  │  HTTP)      │  │                     │  │
│  └──────┬──────┘  └──────┬──────┘  └──────────┬──────────┘  │
│         │                │                    │              │
│         └────────────────┼────────────────────┘              │
│                          │                                    │
│                          ▼                                    │
│  ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐  │
│  │              Tool Dispatcher (统一分发)                 │  │
│  │  - 权限检查 (前置)  - 超时控制  - 重试  - 审计          │  │
│  └────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────┘  │
│                           │                                    │
│  ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐  │
│  │              Bridges (外部桥接)                          │  │
│  │  ┌──────┐ ┌────────┐ ┌──────────┐ ┌──────────────┐    │  │
│  │  │ LSP  │ │Editor  │ │Terminal  │ │Browser       │    │  │
│  │  │Bridge│ │Bridge  │ │Bridge    │ │Bridge        │    │  │
│  │  └──────┘ └────────┘ └──────────┘ └──────────────┘    │  │
│  └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘  │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

### 9.2 Tool Registry (工具注册中心)

#### 11.2.1 接口定义

```typescript
// src/capabilities/tools/types.ts
export interface Tool {
  readonly id: ToolId;
  readonly name: string;
  readonly description: string;
  readonly manifest: ToolManifest;
  readonly schema: ToolSchema;            // JSON Schema for parameters
  readonly permissions: ToolPermission[];
  readonly category: ToolCategory;
  readonly version: string;
  readonly deprecated?: boolean;

  execute(params: unknown, ctx: ToolContext): Promise<ToolResult>;
  validate(params: unknown): ValidationResult;
  estimateCost?(params: unknown): CostEstimate;
  dryRun?(params: unknown, ctx: ToolContext): Promise<DryRunResult>;
}

export interface ToolManifest {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  version: string;
  description: string;
  category: 'file' | 'shell' | 'git' | 'search' | 'network' | 'mcp' | 'custom';
  inputSchema: JSONSchema;
  outputSchema?: JSONSchema;
  permissions: PermissionSpec[];
  rateLimit?: { calls: number; windowMs: number };
  timeout?: number;
  retryable?: boolean;
  destructive?: boolean;
  requiresConfirmation?: 'always' | 'on_change' | 'never';
}

export interface ToolSchema {
  input: JSONSchema;
  output?: JSONSchema;
}

export interface ToolContext {
  sessionId: string;
  taskId: string;
  workingDir: string;
  permissions: GrantedPermission[];
  timeout: number;
  abortSignal: AbortSignal;
  env: Record<string, string>;
  logger: Logger;
  emit: (event: ToolEvent) => void;
}

export interface ToolResult {
  ok: boolean;
  output: unknown;
  error?: ToolError;
  metadata?: {
    durationMs: number;
    tokensUsed?: number;
    bytesWritten?: number;
    filesChanged?: string[];
  };
}

export type ToolCategory =
  | 'filesystem'  // 文件读写
  | 'shell'       // 命令执行
  | 'git'         // 版本控制
  | 'search'      // 代码搜索
  | 'network'     // 网络请求
  | 'mcp'         // MCP 工具
  | 'plugin'      // 插件工具
  | 'custom';     // 自定义
```

#### 11.2.2 ToolRegistry 实现

```typescript
// src/capabilities/tools/registry.ts
export class ToolRegistry {
  private tools = new Map<ToolId, Tool>();
  private byCategory = new Map<ToolCategory, Tool[]>();
  private byName = new Map<string, ToolId>();
  private versionIndex = new Map<string, Set<string>>();

  register(tool: Tool): void {
    // 1. 校验 manifest
    const validation = this.validateManifest(tool.manifest);
    if (!validation.valid) {
      throw new Error(`Invalid manifest: ${validation.errors.join(', ')}`);
    }
    // 2. 检查 ID 冲突
    if (this.tools.has(tool.id) && !this.isOverwriteAllowed(tool.id)) {
      throw new Error(`Tool already registered: ${tool.id}`);
    }
    // 3. 注册
    this.tools.set(tool.id, tool);
    this.byName.set(tool.name, tool.id);
    this.addToCategory(tool);
    this.emit('registered', { toolId: tool.id, version: tool.version });
  }

  unregister(toolId: ToolId): void {
    const tool = this.tools.get(toolId);
    if (!tool) return;
    this.tools.delete(toolId);
    this.byName.delete(tool.name);
    this.removeFromCategory(tool);
    this.emit('unregistered', { toolId });
  }

  resolve(nameOrId: string): Tool | undefined {
    if (this.tools.has(nameOrId as ToolId)) {
      return this.tools.get(nameOrId as ToolId);
    }
    const id = this.byName.get(nameOrId);
    return id ? this.tools.get(id) : undefined;
  }

  listByCategory(category: ToolCategory): Tool[] {
    return this.byCategory.get(category) ?? [];
  }

  listAll(): Tool[] {
    return [...this.tools.values()];
  }

  /** 为 LLM 生成 tools 数组 (function calling schema) */
  toFunctionCallingSchema(filter?: (t: Tool) => boolean): FunctionSpec[] {
    const tools = filter ? [...this.tools.values()].filter(filter) : [...this.tools.values()];
    return tools.map(t => ({
      type: 'function',
      function: {
        name: t.name,
        description: t.description,
        parameters: t.schema.input,
      },
    }));
  }

  private validateManifest(m: ToolManifest): ValidationResult {
    const errors: string[] = [];
    if (!m.id) errors.push('id required');
    if (!m.name) errors.push('name required');
    if (!m.inputSchema) errors.push('inputSchema required');
    if (m.destructive && m.requiresConfirmation === 'never') {
      errors.push('destructive tools must require confirmation');
    }
    return { valid: errors.length === 0, errors };
  }
}
```

### 9.3 Tool Dispatcher (统一分发)

```typescript
// src/capabilities/tools/dispatcher.ts
export class ToolDispatcher {
  constructor(
    private registry: ToolRegistry,
    private permissionGateway: PermissionGateway,
    private auditLog: AuditLogger,
    private eventBus: EventBus,
    private workerPool: ToolWorkerPool,
  ) {}

  async dispatch(call: ToolCall, ctx: SessionContext): Promise<ToolResult> {
    const tool = this.registry.resolve(call.toolName);
    if (!tool) {
      return this.fail(call, `Unknown tool: ${call.toolName}`);
    }

    // 1. 权限检查
    const permCheck = await this.permissionGateway.check({
      tool,
      params: call.params,
      ctx,
    });
    if (!permCheck.allowed) {
      await this.auditLog.record({ type: 'tool_denied', call, reason: permCheck.reason });
      return this.fail(call, `Permission denied: ${permCheck.reason}`);
    }

    // 2. 参数校验
    const validation = tool.validate(call.params);
    if (!validation.valid) {
      return this.fail(call, `Invalid params: ${validation.errors.join(', ')}`);
    }

    // 3. HITL 确认 (如需要)
    if (this.needsConfirmation(tool, call)) {
      const approved = await this.requestHumanApproval(call, tool, ctx);
      if (!approved) {
        return this.fail(call, 'User rejected');
      }
    }

    // 4. 派发到 Worker 执行
    const toolContext = this.buildContext(call, ctx);
    const result = await this.workerPool.execute(async () => {
      return tool.execute(call.params, toolContext);
    }, { timeout: tool.manifest.timeout ?? 30000 });

    // 5. 审计 + 事件
    await this.auditLog.record({ type: 'tool_executed', call, result });
    this.eventBus.emit('tool.executed', { call, result });

    return result;
  }

  private needsConfirmation(tool: Tool, call: ToolCall): boolean {
    if (tool.manifest.requiresConfirmation === 'always') return true;
    if (tool.manifest.requiresConfirmation === 'never') return false;
    return tool.manifest.destructive === true;
  }
}
```

### 9.4 MCP Client (Model Context Protocol 客户端)

```typescript
// src/capabilities/mcp/client.ts
import { Client } from '@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/client/index.js';
import { StdioClientTransport } from '@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/client/stdio.js';
import { SSEClientTransport } from '@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/client/sse.js';
import { StreamableHTTPClientTransport } from '@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/client/streamableHttp.js';

export type McpTransportKind = 'stdio' | 'sse' | 'http';

export interface McpServerConfig {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  transport: McpTransportKind;
  command?: string;        // stdio
  args?: string[];         // stdio
  url?: string;            // sse | http
  env?: Record<string, string>;
  enabled: boolean;
  autoStart: boolean;
  timeout?: number;
}

export class McpClient {
  private connections = new Map<string, McpConnection>();
  private toolsCache = new Map<string, Tool[]>();

  async connect(config: McpServerConfig): Promise<void> {
    if (this.connections.has(config.id)) {
      throw new Error(`MCP server already connected: ${config.id}`);
    }
    const transport = this.createTransport(config);
    const client = new Client({ name: 'OpenWork-agent', version: '1.0.0' }, { capabilities: {} });
    await client.connect(transport);

    const connection: McpConnection = { config, client, transport, status: 'connected' };
    this.connections.set(config.id, connection);

    // 拉取工具清单
    const tools = await this.listTools(config.id);
    this.toolsCache.set(config.id, tools);
    this.emit('connected', { serverId: config.id, toolCount: tools.length });
  }

  async disconnect(serverId: string): Promise<void> {
    const conn = this.connections.get(serverId);
    if (!conn) return;
    await conn.client.close();
    this.connections.delete(serverId);
    this.toolsCache.delete(serverId);
    this.emit('disconnected', { serverId });
  }

  async listTools(serverId: string): Promise<Tool[]> {
    const conn = this.connections.get(serverId);
    if (!conn) throw new Error(`Not connected: ${serverId}`);
    const { tools } = await conn.client.listTools();
    return tools.map(t => this.adaptMcpTool(serverId, t));
  }

  async callTool(serverId: string, name: string, args: unknown): Promise<unknown> {
    const conn = this.connections.get(serverId);
    if (!conn) throw new Error(`Not connected: ${serverId}`);
    const result = await conn.client.callTool({ name, arguments: args as Record<string, unknown> });
    return result;
  }

  private createTransport(config: McpServerConfig) {
    switch (config.transport) {
      case 'stdio':
        return new StdioClientTransport({
          command: config.command!,
          args: config.args ?? [],
          env: { ...process.env, ...config.env },
        });
      case 'sse':
        return new SSEClientTransport(new URL(config.url!));
      case 'http':
        return new StreamableHTTPClientTransport(new URL(config.url!));
      default:
        throw new Error(`Unknown transport: ${config.transport}`);
    }
  }

  /** 将 MCP tool 适配为内部 Tool 接口 */
  private adaptMcpTool(serverId: string, mcpTool: unknown): Tool {
    // ... 适配逻辑
    return mcpTool as Tool;
  }
}

interface McpConnection {
  config: McpServerConfig;
  client: Client;
  transport: unknown;
  status: 'connected' | 'disconnected' | 'error';
}
```

### 9.5 Plugin Sandbox (插件沙箱)

```typescript
// src/capabilities/plugins/sandbox.ts
import ivm from 'isolated-vm';

export class PluginSandbox {
  private isolate: ivm.Isolate;
  private context: ivm.Context;
  private pluginManifests = new Map<string, PluginManifest>();

  constructor(private memoryLimitMB: number = 128) {
    this.isolate = new ivm.Isolate({ memoryLimit: this.memoryLimitMB });
    this.context = this.isolate.createContextSync();
    this.setupSandboxGlobal();
  }

  async loadPlugin(manifest: PluginManifest, code: string): Promise<void> {
    // 1. 校验 manifest
    this.validateManifest(manifest);
    // 2. 在沙箱内执行插件代码
    const script = await this.isolate.compileScript(code, { filename: manifest.id });
    await script.run(this.context);
    // 3. 注册 manifest
    this.pluginManifests.set(manifest.id, manifest);
  }

  async invoke(pluginId: string, method: string, args: unknown[]): Promise<unknown> {
    const manifest = this.pluginManifests.get(pluginId);
    if (!manifest) throw new Error(`Plugin not loaded: ${pluginId}`);

    // 通过 isolate 的 applySync 函数调用插件方法
    const fnRef = await this.context.global.get(method);
    if (!fnRef || typeof fnRef !== 'function') {
      throw new Error(`Method not found: ${method}`);
    }
    return fnRef.applySync(undefined, args.map(a => new ivm.ExternalCopy(a).copyIn()));
  }

  /** 暴露给沙箱的安全 API (白名单) */
  private setupSandboxGlobal(): void {
    const api = {
      console: (msg: string) => this.emit('log', msg),
      fetch: (url: string) => this.handleSandboxFetch(url),
      readFile: (path: string) => this.handleSandboxReadFile(path),
      writeFile: (path: string, content: string) => this.handleSandboxWriteFile(path, content),
    };
    this.context.global.setSync('openwork', api);
  }
}

interface PluginManifest {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  version: string;
  author: string;
  description: string;
  permissions: PluginPermission[];
  hooks?: HookSpec[];
  tools?: ToolManifest[];
}
```

### 9.5.1 isolated-vm V8 双编译风险与回退方案 (v4.2 新增, S6)

> **v4.2 变更** (S6): 增加 isolated-vm V8 版本耦合风险小节与回退方案。isolated-vm 依赖 Node.js 内置 V8 版本，Electron 升级时 V8 版本不匹配会导致编译失败，是已知的 Electron 项目痛点。

#### 风险描述

| 风险 | 影响 | 触发场景 |
|------|------|----------|
| **V8 版本耦合** | isolated-vm 必须与运行时 V8 版本严格匹配，否则编译失败 (node-gyp 报 ABI 不兼容) | Node.js 小版本升级 (如 22.4 → 22.5) |
| **Electron V8 双编译** | 嵌入式模式后端跑在 Electron Main 进程 (用 Electron V8)；服务式模式后端跑在独立 Node.js 进程 (用 Node.js V8)。两种模式需**不同编译产物**的 isolated-vm 二进制 | 嵌入式 ↔ 服务式切换、Electron 升级 |
| **electron-rebuild 依赖** | 嵌入式模式需 `electron-rebuild` 重新编译 isolated-vm 针对 Electron V8 头文件 | Electron 版本升级 |
| **ABI 兼容窗口窄** | isolated-vm 新版通常滞后 Node.js/Electron 1-2 个小版本，升级窗口存在「无可用 isolated-vm 版本」的真空期 | Node.js/Electron 刚发布新版本 |

#### 双编译产物说明

```
嵌入式模式 (v0.1, Electron Main 进程):
  ─ 运行时 V8: Electron 35 内置 V8 (与 Node.js 22 LTS 的 V8 版本不同)
  ─ 编译方式: electron-rebuild --module-dir node_modules/isolated-vm
  ─ 产物: isolated-vm.node (Electron V8 ABI)

服务式模式 (v0.2+, 独立 Node.js 进程):
  ─ 运行时 V8: Node.js 22 LTS 内置 V8
  ─ 编译方式: npm rebuild isolated-vm (或 prebuild-install)
  ─ 产物: isolated-vm.node (Node.js V8 ABI)

⚠ 两种产物不通用，monorepo 需在 build 脚本中区分目标运行时
```

#### 回退方案 (分级降级)

| 优先级 | 方案 | 隔离强度 | 性能 | 适用场景 |
|:---:|------|----------|------|----------|
| 1 (首选) | **isolated-vm** | 强 (独立 V8 Isolate，无 fs/network) | 高 | 正常情况，V8 版本匹配 |
| 2 (回退) | **worker_threads + `node:vm` 模块** | 中 (Worker 进程隔离 + vm 沙箱，但 vm 可访问 require) | 中 | isolated-vm 编译失败时自动回退 |
| 3 (远期) | **QuickJS / wasm-micro-runtime** | 强 (WASM 沙箱，与宿主 V8 完全解耦) | 低 | 彻底解决 V8 耦合，v1.0+ 评估 |

```typescript
// packages/core/src/plugins/sandbox-factory.ts (v4.2 新增)
import type { PluginSandboxInterface } from '@openwork/api';

export async function createSandbox(memoryLimitMB: number): Promise<PluginSandboxInterface> {
  // 1. 优先尝试 isolated-vm
  try {
    const { PluginSandbox } = await import('./isolated-vm-sandbox');
    return new PluginSandbox(memoryLimitMB);
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('[PluginSandbox] isolated-vm 不可用，回退到 worker_threads + vm', err);
  }
  // 2. 回退: worker_threads + node:vm
  const { WorkerVmSandbox } = await import('./worker-vm-sandbox');
  return new WorkerVmSandbox(memoryLimitMB);
}
```

#### 缓解措施

1. **CI 矩阵测试**: CI 中同时测试 isolated-vm 在 Node.js 22 LTS 与 Electron 35 下的编译与运行；
2. **版本锁定**: `package.json` 锁定 isolated-vm 版本，升级 Node.js/Electron 前先验证 isolated-vm 兼容性；
3. **启动探测**: 后端启动时探测 isolated-vm 是否可用 (尝试创建空 Isolate)，失败则自动回退到 worker_threads + vm，并记录告警；
4. **文档同步**: 05-技术选型文档 §12 与 06-详细设计文档 §3.10 同步说明双编译要求与回退策略。

### 9.6 Bridges (桥接器)

```typescript
// src/capabilities/bridges/types.ts
export interface Bridge {
  readonly kind: BridgeKind;
  start(): Promise<void>;
  stop(): Promise<void>;
  isAlive(): boolean;
}

export type BridgeKind = 'lsp' | 'editor' | 'terminal' | 'browser';

// LSP Bridge — 与 Language Server Protocol 通信
export interface LspBridge extends Bridge {
  kind: 'lsp';
  openDocument(uri: string, text: string): Promise<void>;
  getDiagnostics(uri: string): Promise<Diagnostic[]>;
  getCompletions(uri: string, position: Position): Promise<Completion[]>;
  getDefinition(uri: string, position: Position): Promise<Location[]>;
  getHover(uri: string, position: Position): Promise<Hover | null>;
  formatDocument(uri: string): Promise<TextEdit[]>;
}

// Editor Bridge — 与 Monaco Editor 通信
export interface EditorBridge extends Bridge {
  kind: 'editor';
  openFile(path: string): Promise<void>;
  applyEdit(edit: FileEdit): Promise<void>;
  getSelection(): EditorSelection | null;
  scrollToPosition(position: Position): void;
  setDecorations(decorations: Decoration[]): void;
}

// Terminal Bridge — 与 xterm.js 通信
export interface TerminalBridge extends Bridge {
  kind: 'terminal';
  execute(cmd: string): Promise<TerminalResult>;
  getCwd(): string;
  onOutput(cb: (data: string) => void): void;
  resize(cols: number, rows: number): void;
}

// Browser Bridge — 与内置浏览器 (自动化) 通信
export interface BrowserBridge extends Bridge {
  kind: 'browser';
  navigate(url: string): Promise<void>;
  screenshot(): Promise<Buffer>;
  evaluate(script: string): Promise<unknown>;
  click(selector: string): Promise<void>;
  fillForm(selector: string, value: string): Promise<void>;
}
```

### 9.7 关键设计决策

| 决策 ID | 决策 | 理由 |
|---------|------|------|
| **D7.1** | 工具执行与 LLM 推理在不同 Worker | 崩溃隔离 + 并行 |
| **D7.2** | MCP 三种 transport 全支持 | 生态兼容性最大化 |
| **D7.3** | 插件使用 isolated-vm 而非 Worker | 内存隔离更强 + 启动更快 |
| **D7.4** | Bridge 抽象统一接口 | LSP/Editor/Terminal/Browser 可替换实现 |
| **D7.5** | ToolDispatcher 强制经权限网关 | 安全合规 |
| **D7.6** | 所有工具调用必落审计日志 | 不可擦除审计 |
| **D7.7** | MCP 工具缓存到本地 | 减少重复 listTools 调用 |

---

## 10. 模型抽象层架构

### 10.1 层级定位

模型抽象层 (Layer 8) 将不同 LLM 供应商的 SDK 差异屏蔽，向上提供统一的 `Provider` 接口。它运行于后端进程的 Agent Worker 内，无独立进程。

```
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│              模型抽象层 (Model Abstraction) L5                │
│                                                                │
│  ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐  │
│  │              Provider Router (路由器)                   │  │
│  │  - 按模型 ID 路由  - 按能力路由  - 按成本路由            │  │
│  └────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────┘  │
│                           │                                    │
│  ┌────────────────────────▼───────────────────────────────┐  │
│  │              Provider Adapter (适配器)                  │  │
│  │  ┌────────┐ ┌──────────┐ ┌────────┐ ┌──────────────┐  │  │
│  │  │OpenAI │ │Anthropic │ │Ollama  │ │Custom        │  │  │
│  │  │        │ │          │ │        │ │(OpenAI-compat)│  │  │
│  │  └────────┘ └──────────┘ └────────┘ └──────────────┘  │  │
│  └────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────┘  │
│                           │                                    │
│  ┌────────────────────────▼───────────────────────────────┐  │
│  │              Cost Controller (成本控制)                │  │
│  │  - 预算检查  - 累计统计  - 熔断  - 告警                 │  │
│  └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘  │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

### 10.2 Provider 接口

```typescript
// src/models/provider.ts
export interface Provider {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly name: string;
  readonly kind: ProviderKind;
  readonly capabilities: ProviderCapabilities;
  readonly models: ModelSpec[];

  chat(req: ChatRequest): Promise<ChatResponse>;
  chatStream(req: ChatRequest): AsyncIterable<ChatChunk>;
  embed(req: EmbedRequest): Promise<EmbedResponse>;
  countTokens(text: string, model: string): number;
  estimateCost(usage: TokenUsage, model: string): number;
  healthCheck(): Promise<HealthStatus>;
}

export type ProviderKind = 'openai' | 'anthropic' | 'ollama' | 'custom' | 'azure-openai';

export interface ProviderCapabilities {
  functionCalling: boolean;
  vision: boolean;
  audio: boolean;
  streaming: boolean;
  jsonMode: boolean;
  parallelToolCalls: boolean;
  longContext: boolean;
  maxContextTokens: number;
}

export interface ModelSpec {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  contextWindow: number;
  inputCostPer1K?: number;   // USD
  outputCostPer1K?: number;
  capabilities: Partial<ProviderCapabilities>;
}

export interface ChatRequest {
  model: string;
  messages: ChatMessage[];
  tools?: FunctionSpec[];
  temperature?: number;
  maxTokens?: number;
  topP?: number;
  stop?: string[];
  stream?: boolean;
  jsonMode?: boolean;
  responseFormat?: 'text' | 'json_object' | 'json_schema';
  jsonSchema?: JSONSchema;
  system?: string;
  user?: string;
  metadata?: Record<string, unknown>;
}

export interface ChatResponse {
  id: string;
  model: string;
  content: string;
  toolCalls?: ToolCall[];
  finishReason: 'stop' | 'length' | 'tool_calls' | 'content_filter';
  usage: TokenUsage;
  cost: number;  // USD
}

export interface ChatChunk {
  delta?: string;
  toolCallDelta?: ToolCallDelta;
  finishReason?: string;
  usage?: TokenUsage;
}

export interface TokenUsage {
  promptTokens: number;
  completionTokens: number;
  totalTokens: number;
}
```

### 10.3 OpenAI Provider 适配器 (示例)

```typescript
// src/models/providers/openai.ts
import OpenAI from 'openai';
import type { Provider, ChatRequest, ChatResponse, ChatChunk } from '../provider';

export class OpenAIProvider implements Provider {
  readonly id = 'openai';
  readonly name = 'OpenAI';
  readonly kind = 'openai' as const;
  private client: OpenAI;

  constructor(apiKey: string, baseURL?: string) {
    this.client = new OpenAI({ apiKey, baseURL });
  }

  get capabilities() {
    return {
      functionCalling: true,
      vision: true,
      audio: false,
      streaming: true,
      jsonMode: true,
      parallelToolCalls: true,
      longContext: true,
      maxContextTokens: 128000,
    };
  }

  get models() {
    return [
      { id: 'gpt-4o', name: 'GPT-4o', contextWindow: 128000, inputCostPer1K: 0.0025, outputCostPer1K: 0.01, capabilities: { vision: true } },
      { id: 'gpt-4o-mini', name: 'GPT-4o Mini', contextWindow: 128000, inputCostPer1K: 0.00015, outputCostPer1K: 0.0006 },
      { id: 'o1', name: 'o1', contextWindow: 200000, inputCostPer1K: 0.015, outputCostPer1K: 0.06, capabilities: { vision: true } },
    ];
  }

  async chat(req: ChatRequest): Promise<ChatResponse> {
    const resp = await this.client.chat.completions.create({
      model: req.model,
      messages: req.messages.map(this.toOpenAIMessage),
      tools: req.tools?.map(this.toOpenAITool),
      temperature: req.temperature,
      max_tokens: req.maxTokens,
      stream: false,
      response_format: req.jsonMode ? { type: 'json_object' } : undefined,
    });
    return this.fromOpenAI(resp);
  }

  async *chatStream(req: ChatRequest): AsyncIterable<ChatChunk> {
    const stream = await this.client.chat.completions.create({
      model: req.model,
      messages: req.messages.map(this.toOpenAIMessage),
      tools: req.tools?.map(this.toOpenAITool),
      temperature: req.temperature,
      max_tokens: req.maxTokens,
      stream: true,
      stream_options: { include_usage: true },
    });
    for await (const chunk of stream) {
      yield this.toChatChunk(chunk);
    }
  }

  // ... 其他方法省略
}
```

### 10.4 Provider Router (路由器)

```typescript
// src/models/router.ts
export class ProviderRouter {
  private providers = new Map<string, Provider>();
  private fallbackChain = new Map<string, string[]>(); // modelId -> provider chain
  private costController: CostController;

  registerProvider(provider: Provider): void {
    this.providers.set(provider.id, provider);
  }

  setFallbackChain(modelId: string, chain: string[]): void {
    this.fallbackChain.set(modelId, chain);
  }

  async chat(req: ChatRequest): Promise<ChatResponse> {
    const chain = this.fallbackChain.get(req.model) ?? [req.model];
    let lastError: Error | undefined;

    for (const modelId of chain) {
      const provider = this.resolveProvider(modelId);
      if (!provider) continue;
      // 预算检查
      if (!this.costController.canSpend(req.user ?? 'default', 0.5)) {
        throw new Error('Cost budget exceeded');
      }
      try {
        const response = await provider.chat({ ...req, model: modelId });
        this.costController.recordSpend(req.user ?? 'default', response.cost);
        return response;
      } catch (e) {
        lastError = e as Error;
        this.emit('fallback', { from: modelId, error: lastError.message });
        continue;
      }
    }
    throw lastError ?? new Error('No provider available');
  }

  private resolveProvider(modelId: string): Provider | undefined {
    for (const p of this.providers.values()) {
      if (p.models.some(m => m.id === modelId)) return p;
    }
    return undefined;
  }
}
```

### 10.5 Cost Controller (成本控制器)

```typescript
// src/models/cost-controller.ts
export class CostController {
  private spendByUser = new Map<string, number>();
  private spendBySession = new Map<string, number>();
  private budgetByUser = new Map<string, number>();
  private circuitBreakers = new Map<string, CircuitBreakerState>();

  canSpend(userId: string, estimatedCost: number): boolean {
    const budget = this.budgetByUser.get(userId);
    if (!budget) return true;
    const spent = this.spendByUser.get(userId) ?? 0;
    if (spent + estimatedCost > budget) {
      this.emit('budget_exceeded', { userId, spent, budget });
      return false;
    }
    return true;
  }

  recordSpend(userId: string, cost: number, sessionId?: string): void {
    this.spendByUser.set(userId, (this.spendByUser.get(userId) ?? 0) + cost);
    if (sessionId) {
      this.spendBySession.set(sessionId, (this.spendBySession.get(sessionId) ?? 0) + cost);
    }
  }

  setBudget(userId: string, budget: number): void {
    this.budgetByUser.set(userId, budget);
  }

  getSpend(userId: string): { spent: number; budget?: number; remaining?: number } {
    const spent = this.spendByUser.get(userId) ?? 0;
    const budget = this.budgetByUser.get(userId);
    return budget ? { spent, budget, remaining: budget - spent } : { spent };
  }
}
```

### 10.6 关键设计决策

| 决策 ID | 决策 | 理由 |
|---------|------|------|
| **D8.1** | Provider 接口统一 streaming / 非流式 | 上层无需关心差异 |
| **D8.2** | 路由器支持 fallback 链 | 提升高可用 |
| **D8.3** | 成本控制器独立于 Provider | 单一职责 |
| **D8.4** | 预算检查前置 (预估 + 实扣) | 防止超支 |
| **D8.5** | 不内置 Rate Limiting，依赖 SDK | SDK 已有重试机制 |

---

## 11. 基础设施层架构

### 11.1 层级定位

基础设施层 (Layer 9) 是所有上层模块共享的工具层，包括 SQLite、向量索引、FTS5、加密、IPC、Event Bus、日志等。

```
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                基础设施层 (Infrastructure) L6                  │
│                                                                │
│  ┌──────────────┐ ┌──────────────┐ ┌──────────────────────┐  │
│  │ SQLite       │ │ Vector Index │ │ Full-Text Search     │  │
│  │ (better-     │ │ (sqlite-vec) │ │ (FTS5)               │  │
│  │  sqlite3)    │ │              │ │                      │  │
│  └──────────────┘ └──────────────┘ └──────────────────────┘  │
│                                                                │
│  ┌──────────────┐ ┌──────────────┐ ┌──────────────────────┐  │
│  │ Crypto       │ │ IPC Router   │ │ Event Bus            │  │
│  │ (node:crypto)│ │ (Electron)   │ │ (EventEmitter)       │  │
│  └──────────────┘ └──────────────┘ └──────────────────────┘  │
│                                                                │
│  ┌──────────────┐ ┌──────────────┐ ┌──────────────────────┐  │
│  │ Logger       │ │ File Watcher │ │ Updater              │  │
│  │ (pino)       │ │ (chokidar)   │ │ (electron-updater)   │  │
│  └──────────────┘ └──────────────┘ └──────────────────────┘  │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

### 11.2 SQLite 数据库层

```typescript
// src/infra/db/database.ts
import Database from 'better-sqlite3';

export class Database {
  private db: Database.Database;
  private preparedStatements = new Map<string, Database.Statement>();

  constructor(private dbPath: string) {
    this.db = new Database(dbPath);
    this.db.pragma('journal_mode = WAL');
    this.db.pragma('foreign_keys = ON');
    this.db.pragma('synchronous = NORMAL');
    this.db.pragma('busy_timeout = 5000');
    this.loadExtensions();
  }

  private loadExtensions(): void {
    // 加载 sqlite-vec 扩展
    this.db.loadExtension('vec0');
  }

  prepare(key: string, sql: string): Database.Statement {
    if (!this.preparedStatements.has(key)) {
      this.preparedStatements.set(key, this.db.prepare(sql));
    }
    return this.preparedStatements.get(key)!;
  }

  transaction<T>(fn: () => T): T {
    return this.db.transaction(fn)();
  }

  exec(sql: string): void {
    this.db.exec(sql);
  }

  close(): void {
    this.preparedStatements.clear();
    this.db.close();
  }
}
```

### 11.3 向量索引

```typescript
// src/infra/db/vector-index.ts
export class VectorIndex {
  constructor(private db: Database) {
    this.initSchema();
  }

  private initSchema(): void {
    this.db.exec(`
      CREATE VIRTUAL TABLE IF NOT EXISTS code_chunks_vec
      USING vec0(
        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        embedding FLOAT[1536]
      );
    `);
  }

  upsert(id: string, embedding: number[]): void {
    const stmt = this.db.prepare(
      'vec-upsert',
      'INSERT OR REPLACE INTO code_chunks_vec (id, embedding) VALUES (?, ?)',
    );
    stmt.run(id, JSON.stringify(embedding));
  }

  search(queryEmbedding: number[], limit: number = 10): VectorMatch[] {
    const stmt = this.db.prepare(
      'vec-search',
      'SELECT id, distance FROM code_chunks_vec WHERE embedding MATCH ? ORDER BY distance LIMIT ?',
    );
    return stmt.all(JSON.stringify(queryEmbedding), limit) as VectorMatch[];
  }
}

interface VectorMatch {
  id: string;
  distance: number;
}
```

### 11.4 FTS5 全文索引

```typescript
// src/infra/db/fts-index.ts
export class FtsIndex {
  constructor(private db: Database) {
    this.initSchema();
  }

  private initSchema(): void {
    this.db.exec(`
      CREATE VIRTUAL TABLE IF NOT EXISTS code_search_fts
      USING fts5(
        id UNINDEXED,
        path,
        content,
        language,
        tokenize = 'trigram'
      );
    `);
  }

  index(id: string, path: string, content: string, language: string): void {
    const stmt = this.db.prepare(
      'fts-index',
      'INSERT INTO code_search_fts (id, path, content, language) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)',
    );
    stmt.run(id, path, content, language);
  }

  search(query: string, limit: number = 20): FtsMatch[] {
    const stmt = this.db.prepare(
      'fts-search',
      `SELECT id, path, snippet(code_search_fts, 2, '<mark>', '</mark>', '...', 20) as snippet, rank
       FROM code_search_fts
       WHERE content MATCH ?
       ORDER BY rank
       LIMIT ?`,
    );
    return stmt.all(query, limit) as FtsMatch[];
  }
}
```

### 11.5 Crypto 加密层

```typescript
// src/infra/crypto/crypto.ts
import { createCipheriv, createDecipheriv, randomBytes, scryptSync } from 'node:crypto';

export class CryptoService {
  private algorithm = 'aes-256-gcm';

  encrypt(plaintext: string, password: string): EncryptedData {
    const salt = randomBytes(16);
    const key = scryptSync(password, salt, 32);
    const iv = randomBytes(12);
    const cipher = createCipheriv(this.algorithm, key, iv);
    const encrypted = Buffer.concat([cipher.update(plaintext, 'utf8'), cipher.final()]);
    const authTag = cipher.getAuthTag();
    return { salt, iv, encrypted, authTag };
  }

  decrypt(data: EncryptedData, password: string): string {
    const key = scryptSync(password, data.salt, 32);
    const decipher = createDecipheriv(this.algorithm, key, data.iv);
    decipher.setAuthTag(data.authTag);
    return decipher.update(data.encrypted, undefined, 'utf8') + decipher.final('utf8');
  }
}

interface EncryptedData {
  salt: Buffer;
  iv: Buffer;
  encrypted: Buffer;
  authTag: Buffer;
}
```

### 11.6 IPC Router

```typescript
// src/infra/ipc/router.ts
import { ipcMain, ipcRenderer, BrowserWindow } from 'electron';

export class IpcRouter {
  private handlers = new Map<string, IpcHandler>();

  register(channel: string, handler: IpcHandler): void {
    if (this.handlers.has(channel)) {
      throw new Error(`Channel already registered: ${channel}`);
    }
    this.handlers.set(channel, handler);
    ipcMain.handle(channel, async (event, ...args) => {
      try {
        const result = await handler(...args);
        return { ok: true, data: result };
      } catch (e) {
        return { ok: false, error: (e as Error).message };
      }
    });
  }

  unregister(channel: string): void {
    ipcMain.removeHandler(channel);
    this.handlers.delete(channel);
  }

  sendToRenderer(window: BrowserWindow, channel: string, data: unknown): void {
    window.webContents.send(channel, data);
  }
}

type IpcHandler = (...args: unknown[]) => Promise<unknown> | unknown;
```

### 11.7 Logger 日志层

```typescript
// src/infra/logger/logger.ts
import pino from 'pino';

export class Logger {
  private pinoLogger: pino.Logger;

  constructor(private level: LogLevel = 'info') {
    this.pinoLogger = pino({
      level,
      transport: {
        targets: [
          { target: 'pino-pretty', level, options: { colorize: true } },
          { target: 'pino/file', level, options: { destination: 'logs/app.log', mkdir: true } },
        ],
      },
    });
  }

  child(bindings: Record<string, unknown>): Logger {
    const childLogger = new Logger(this.level);
    childLogger.pinoLogger = this.pinoLogger.child(bindings);
    return childLogger;
  }

  info(msg: string, meta?: Record<string, unknown>): void { this.pinoLogger.info(meta ?? {}, msg); }
  warn(msg: string, meta?: Record<string, unknown>): void { this.pinoLogger.warn(meta ?? {}, msg); }
  error(msg: string, meta?: Record<string, unknown>): void { this.pinoLogger.error(meta ?? {}, msg); }
  debug(msg: string, meta?: Record<string, unknown>): void { this.pinoLogger.debug(meta ?? {}, msg); }
}

type LogLevel = 'trace' | 'debug' | 'info' | 'warn' | 'error' | 'fatal';
```

### 11.8 关键设计决策

| 决策 ID | 决策 | 理由 |
|---------|------|------|
| **D9.1** | SQLite + WAL 模式 | 读写并发 + 崩溃安全 |
| **D9.2** | 预编译语句缓存 | 性能 |
| **D9.3** | sqlite-vec 而非 LanceDB (默认) | 减少依赖 + 同进程内查询 |
| **D9.4** | FTS5 + trigram 分词 | 支持中文 + 无需外部词典 |
| **D9.5** | AES-256-GCM 用于 API Key 加密 | 行业标准 |
| **D9.6** | pino 而非 winston | 性能 + JSON 友好 |
| **D9.7** | IPC 全部走 contextBridge | 安全 (contextIsolation) |
| **D9.8** (v4.3) | **DDD Repository 接口隔离 + Drizzle ORM 查询构造** | 业务层只依赖 `Repository<T>`，TS 类型安全的 SQL 构造；SQLite 驱动处可替换为 PG/MySQL 而不触业务层 (见 §11.9) |

### 11.9 DDD Repository 契约与实现隔离 (v4.3 新增)

> **目标**: 借助 DDD (Domain-Driven Design) Repository 模式，把持久化的"接口 / 查询 / 操作"与具体驱动 (SQLite / better-sqlite3) 强隔离。所有领域模块只看到接口、看不到 SQL；具体实现属于 Infrastructure 层、可被替换 (为后续 PG/MySQL/远程数据库平滑迁移留出扩展点)。

#### 11.9.1 分层落位

```
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Domain Layer        │ packages/core/src/domain/          │
│ (接口，纯 TS)        │  ├── entity/    (聚合根: Session…)  │
│ 业务模块仅依赖接口  │  ├── repository.ts (Repository<T>) │
│                     │  └── repositories/ (各仓储接口)    │
├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Application Layer   │ packages/core/src/application/     │
│ 用例编排 / 事务边界 │  依赖 RepositoryProvider 注入接口  │
├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Infrastructure      │ packages/core/src/infra/db/        │
│ (实现，驱动耦合)    │  ├── sqlite/  (better-sqlite3 实现)│
│                     │  ├── drizzle/ (查询构造，可选)    │
│                     │  └── RepositoryProvider.ts        │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

#### 11.9.2 关键规则

1. **接口只声明，不实现**：Repository 接口位于 `domain/`，禁止 `import 'better-sqlite3'`；禁止在 domain 层出现 SQL 字符串或驱动类型。
2. **实现可替换**：`Sqlite*Repository` 是 v0.1 实现；后续可提供 `RemoteHttp*Repository` (Service 模式时)、`Postgres*Repository`，只需实现同一接口注入即可。
3. **事务经 `RepositoryProvider.withTx`**：跨聚合事务经 Provider 统一开启，禁止业务层持 raw `Database` 句柄。
4. **查询经查询对象**：`findAll(filter?: RepositoryQuery<T>)` 接受声明式 where/orderBy/limit/offset；复杂查询由各 Repository 接口的特化方法承担 (如 `MessageRepository.findBySessionPaginated`)，禁止业务拼接 SQL。
5. **索引独立访问**：向量 (sqlite-vec) 与 FTS5 通过 `VectorIndexPort` / `FtsIndexPort` 接口暴露，业务用 `searchRelated()` 语义化方法调用，不直接写 SQL。
6. **审计与软删除可插拔**：通过装饰器 / 拦截器在 Infrastructure 层实现 append-only 审计、软删除字段填充，业务接口保持纯净。
7. **可测试性**：领域层可在无 DB 环境下用 InMemoryRepository 做单元测试；集成测试替换为真实 SQLite。

#### 11.9.3 完整接口与聚合根清单

完整 `Repository<T>` 泛型接口、v0.1 落地 12 仓储与 v0.2+ 扩展仓储 (含 v4.3 新增 GOAL/SCHED/PROMPT/PROVIDER 四域) 见 **§16.10**。本节仅给出架构约束与分层落位。

#### 11.9.4 工程目录 (后端持久化基建)

```
packages/core/src/
├── domain/                          # 领域层 (接口)
│   ├── entity/                      # 聚合根类型
│   │   ├── Session.ts  Message.ts  ToolCall.ts  LoopStep.ts
│   │   ├── Config.ts   ConfigHistory.ts  McpServerConfig.ts
│   │   ├── Profile.ts  AuditLogEntry.ts  ModelCall.ts
│   │   ├── Checkpoint.ts ProviderConfig.ts       # v0.1
│   │   ├── Memory.ts HookConfig.ts Skill.ts
│   │   ├── SubAgentConfig.ts CustomCommand.ts IndexMeta.ts  # v0.2+
│   │   ├── Goal.ts ScheduledTask.ts ScheduledTaskRun.ts      # v0.3 新增
│   │   └── PromptTemplate.ts                                # v0.3 新增
│   ├── repository.ts                 # Repository<T> 泛型接口
│   └── repositories/                 # 各聚合根 Repository 接口
│       ├── SessionRepository.ts  MessageRepository.ts  ...
└── infra/db/                         # 基础设施层 (实现)
    ├── sqlite/
    │   ├── SqliteSessionRepository.ts  ...   # better-sqlite3 实现
    │   └── SqliteRepositoryProvider.ts        # withTx + 注入
    ├── drizzle/                               # (可选) schema 与查询构造
    │   ├── schema.ts
    │   └── query.ts
    ├── in-memory/                            # 单元测试 InMemory 实现
    └── ports/                                # 索引 / 外部 IO 接口
        ├── VectorIndexPort.ts
        └── FtsIndexPort.ts
```

#### 11.9.5 与 CS 分离的协同扩展点

CS 分离架构下，TUI/Web/CLI 客户端经 RPC 访问后端 Repository 能力：

- 客户端经 `@openwork/api` 拿到的不是 Repository 直接句柄，而是 **Service (RPC Facade)**，Service 内部调用 Repository。
- 后端 RepositoryProvider 仍是唯一真相源；只读副本能力由 `FindOptions.readOnly` 标记，经 Service 层向只读 RepositoryProvider 分流 (服务式模式 v0.2+ 提供)。
- 承载切换到远程 DB 时，仅需在 `infra/db/` 新增 `Remote*Repository` 实现并切换 Provider 工厂，业务层与客户端零修改。

## 12. Event Bus 与事件系统

---

## 12. Event Bus 与事件系统

### 12.1 事件系统总览

Event Bus 是连接所有模块的神经系统。它支持跨 Worker 通信、订阅/发布模式、背压控制、事件持久化与回放。

```
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                    Event Bus 架构                              │
│                                                                │
│   Main Process          Agent Worker         Tool Worker      │
│   ┌──────────┐          ┌──────────┐         ┌──────────┐    │
│   │ EventBus │◄────────►│EventBus  │◄───────►│EventBus  │    │
│   │  (Hub)   │  msgPort │ (Spoke)  │ msgPort │ (Spoke)  │    │
│   └────┬─────┘          └────┬─────┘         └────┬─────┘    │
│        │                     │                    │           │
│        ▼                     ▼                    ▼           │
│   ┌──────────┐          ┌──────────┐         ┌──────────┐    │
│   │Persistor │          │Subscribers│        │Subscribers│   │
│   │ (SQLite) │          │ (in-proc) │        │ (in-proc) │    │
│   └──────────┘          └──────────┘         └──────────┘    │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

### 12.2 事件类型分类

```typescript
// src/infra/event-bus/types.ts
export type AppEvent =
  | SessionEvent
  | LoopEvent
  | ToolEvent
  | McpEvent
  | PluginEvent
  | PermissionEvent
  | CostEvent
  | SystemEvent;

export interface SessionEvent {
  kind: 'session.created' | 'session.paused' | 'session.resumed' | 'session.completed' | 'session.failed';
  sessionId: string;
  timestamp: number;
  data?: unknown;
}

export interface LoopEvent {
  kind: 'loop.started' | 'loop.step' | 'loop.tool_call' | 'loop.tool_result'
      | 'loop.reflection' | 'loop.checkpoint' | 'loop.completed' | 'loop.error';
  sessionId: string;
  stepId: string;
  timestamp: number;
  data?: unknown;
}

export interface ToolEvent {
  kind: 'tool.dispatched' | 'tool.executing' | 'tool.completed' | 'tool.failed' | 'tool.denied';
  toolCallId: string;
  toolName: string;
  timestamp: number;
  data?: unknown;
}

export interface CostEvent {
  kind: 'cost.recorded' | 'cost.warning' | 'cost.exceeded' | 'cost.budget_set';
  userId?: string;
  sessionId?: string;
  amount: number;
  timestamp: number;
}
```

### 12.3 EventBus 实现

```typescript
// src/infra/event-bus/bus.ts
import { EventEmitter } from 'node:events';
import type { AppEvent } from './types';

export class EventBus extends EventEmitter {
  private persistor?: EventPersistor;
  private backpressureStrategy: BackpressureStrategy = 'drop_oldest';
  private maxQueueSize = 10000;
  private queue: AppEvent[] = [];

  constructor(opts?: { persistor?: EventPersistor; maxQueueSize?: number }) {
    super();
    if (opts?.persistor) this.persistor = opts.persistor;
    if (opts?.maxQueueSize) this.maxQueueSize = opts.maxQueueSize;
  }

  emit(event: AppEvent): boolean {
    // 1. 持久化 (异步,不阻塞)
    if (this.persistor) {
      this.persistor.persist(event).catch(err => {
        this.emitError(err);
      });
    }
    // 2. 背压检查
    if (this.queue.length >= this.maxQueueSize) {
      this.applyBackpressure();
    }
    this.queue.push(event);
    // 3. 派发到本地订阅者
    const result = super.emit(event.kind, event);
    // 4. 派发到通配订阅者
    super.emit('*', event);
    return result;
  }

  subscribe<T extends AppEvent['kind']>(
    kind: T,
    handler: (event: Extract<AppEvent, { kind: T }>) => void,
  ): () => void {
    super.on(kind, handler as (event: AppEvent) => void);
    return () => super.off(kind, handler as (event: AppEvent) => void);
  }

  subscribeAll(handler: (event: AppEvent) => void): () => void {
    super.on('*', handler);
    return () => super.off('*', handler);
  }

  private applyBackpressure(): void {
    switch (this.backpressureStrategy) {
      case 'drop_oldest':
        this.queue.shift();
        break;
      case 'drop_newest':
        this.queue.pop();
        break;
      case 'block':
        // 阻塞 emit (此处简化,实际应异步等待)
        break;
    }
  }
}
```

### 12.4 跨 Worker 事件桥接

```typescript
// src/infra/event-bus/worker-bridge.ts
import { parentPort, MessagePort } from 'node:worker_threads';
import type { AppEvent } from './types';

export class WorkerEventBridge {
  constructor(private port: MessagePort | typeof parentPort) {
    this.port.on('message', (msg: WorkerMessage) => this.handleIncoming(msg));
  }

  forwardToMain(event: AppEvent): void {
    this.port?.postMessage({ kind: 'event', event });
  }

  private handleIncoming(msg: WorkerMessage): void {
    if (msg.kind === 'event') {
      // 转发到本地 EventBus
      localEventBus.emit(msg.event);
    }
  }
}

interface WorkerMessage {
  kind: 'event';
  event: AppEvent;
}
```

### 12.5 事件持久化

```typescript
// src/infra/event-bus/persistor.ts
export class SqliteEventPersistor {
  constructor(private db: Database) {
    this.initSchema();
  }

  private initSchema(): void {
    this.db.exec(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS event_log (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        kind TEXT NOT NULL,
        timestamp INTEGER NOT NULL,
        payload TEXT NOT NULL,
        session_id TEXT,
        created_at INTEGER DEFAULT (unixepoch())
      );
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_event_kind ON event_log(kind);
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_event_session ON event_log(session_id);
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_event_time ON event_log(timestamp);
    `);
  }

  async persist(event: AppEvent): Promise<void> {
    const stmt = this.db.prepare(
      'event-insert',
      'INSERT INTO event_log (kind, timestamp, payload, session_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)',
    );
    stmt.run(
      event.kind,
      event.timestamp,
      JSON.stringify(event),
      (event as { sessionId?: string }).sessionId ?? null,
    );
  }

  replay(sessionId: string, fromTimestamp?: number): AppEvent[] {
    const stmt = this.db.prepare(
      'event-replay',
      'SELECT payload FROM event_log WHERE session_id = ? AND timestamp >= ? ORDER BY timestamp ASC',
    );
    return stmt.all(sessionId, fromTimestamp ?? 0).map((r: { payload: string }) => JSON.parse(r.payload));
  }
}
```

### 12.6 关键设计决策

| 决策 ID | 决策 | 理由 |
|---------|------|------|
| **D10.1** | Main 进程作为 Event Hub | 集中管理 + 持久化 |
| **D10.2** | 跨 Worker 通过 MessagePort | Node.js 原生支持 |
| **D10.3** | 事件持久化到 SQLite | 可回放 + 可观测 |
| **D10.4** | 背压策略可配置 | 不同场景不同策略 |
| **D10.5** | 通配订阅 `*` | 调试器需要全量订阅 |
